> Ntụaka zuru ezu maka environment variable ọ bụla OmniRoute na-amata.
> Maka template mbido-ngwa-ngwa, lee [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Variable ọ bụla edepụtara ebe a ga-apụtakwa na `.env.example`, variable ọ bụla dị na
> `.env.example` ga-apụtakwa ebe a. `npm run check:env-doc-sync`
> na-amanye nke a mgbe a na-eme commit nakwa na CI. Iji kpachapụ variable ụma, tinye ya na
> allowlist dị n'ime `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Ihe Nzuzo Ndị A Chọrọ

A na-emepụta ihe nzuzo ndị edoghị nke akara ha bụ **Ee** na-akpaghị aka n’oge mbido mbụ (`JWT_SECRET`, `API_KEY_SECRET`) ma chekwaa ha na `server.env` n’ime ndekọ data — debe ha n’onwe gị mgbe ndekọ ahụ bụ nke na-adịru nwa oge. Ihe dị iche bụ `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET`: anaghị echekwa ya na `server.env` — ebe mbido sava na-emepụta uru nwa oge pụrụ iche maka mbido ọ bụla nke naanị bridge dị n’ime usoro ahụ nwere ike iji, ya mere ndị ahịa bridge mpụga ga-edobe ya n’onwe ha.

| Agbanwe                      | Achọrọ            | Ndabara                            | Faịlụ Isi                                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ---------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **Ee**            | _(ọ dịghị)_                        | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Na-eji mbinye aka akwado/nyochaa kuki nnọkọ dashboard niile (JWT). Jiri `openssl rand -base64 48` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **Ee**            | _(ọ dịghị)_                        | `src/lib/db/apiKeys.ts`                                   | Igodo izo ezo AES maka uru igodo API ndị echekwara na SQLite. Jiri `openssl rand -hex 32` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | Mba               | _(edoghị)_                         | `src/lib/auth/managementPassword.ts`                      | Na-edobe okwuntughe dashboard n’oge mbido ma ọ bụrụ na echekwabeghị nke ọ bụla; a na-eleghara ya anya ozugbo echekwara okwuntughe. Nwụnye npm na nke sitere na source na-edepụta `.env.example` gaa na `.env`, ya mere ha na-amalite site na placeholder `CHANGEME` nke ọha maara: dochie ya tupu mbido mbụ, ma ọ bụ debe `INITIAL_PASSWORD=` (efu) n’ime `.env` ahụ iji mepụta okwuntughe n’ime onboarding wizard n’oge nleta mbụ, dịka uru edoghị (ndabara Docker image) si eme. Gbanwee okwuntughe echekwara na Dashboard → Ntọala → Nchekwa, ma ọ bụ mee `omniroute reset-password` (`node bin/reset-password.mjs` n’ime source checkout) ebe edobere `DATA_DIR` ka ọ bụrụ ndekọ data sava ahụ. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **Ee** (mmepụta)  | _(edoghị)_                         | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Ihe nzuzo nkekọrịta maka bridge WebSocket nke Codex Responses dị n’ime. Ọ na-enyocha arịrịọ bridge n’etiti relay WS nke Electron/ihe nchọgharị na OmniRoute. ⚠️ **A CHỌRỌ YA na mmepụta — mgbe edoghị ya, a na-ajụ arịrịọ bridge WS niile.** Jiri `openssl rand -base64 32` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | Mba               | _(git SHA)_                        | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | ID doro anya maka ime ka cache service-worker ghara ịdị ochie n’ime shell PWA (#11779); ọ bụ nke mbụ n’usoro mkpebi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | Mba               | _(edoghị)_                         | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Nke abụọ n’usoro ahụ — ndị na-ewu PaaS (dịka ụdị Heroku) na-edobe ya ka ọ bụrụ commit e tinyere n’ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | Mba               | _(ewepụtara)_                      | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Uru ọha nke oge owuwu nke onye ahịa na-eji edebanye `/sw.js?v=…`; a na-ewepụta ya site na abụọ dị n’elu, emesịa git SHA.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | Mba (akpaghị aka) | _(akpaghị aka n’oge mbido ọ bụla)_ | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Ihe nzuzo nke usoro ọ bụla nke na-egosi na stampụ peer-IP a tụkwasịrị obi sitere na sava HTTP nke OmniRoute n’onwe ya (`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). Middleware authz na-atụkwasị mpaghara arịrịọ obi (mgbochi loopback/LAN nke ụzọ LOCAL_ONLY) naanị mgbe stampụ ahụ bu token a. A na-emepụta ya na-akpaghị aka n’oge mbido ọ bụla — hapụ ya ka edoghị ya; naanị debe ya kpọmkwem maka nhazi nwere ọtụtụ usoro ndị ga-ekekọrịta stampụ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUSTED_PROXIES`  | Mba               | _(edoghị)_                         | `scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`                              | IP ma ọ bụ oke CIDR nke reverse proxy ndị e ji rịkọm kewaa, nke ndepụta ikike/mmachibido IP nwere ike ịtụkwasị `X-Forwarded-For` / `X-Real-IP` ha obi, tinyere loopback, adreesị netwọkụ onwe na nsọtụ Cloudflare, nke a na-atụkwasị obi mgbe niile. A chọrọ ya naanị maka proxy dị na adreesị ọha ọzọ ọ bụla; ma ọ bụghị ya, adreesị proxy ahụ n’onwe ya ka nzacha ahụ na-enyocha. Throttle onye ahịa ọ bụla nke relay (`/api/v1/relay/chat/completions`) na-eji otu adreesị onye ahịa ahụ e kpebisiri ike dịka igodo, ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-adabere naanị na headers forwarding.                                                                                                                      |

### Iwu Mepụta

```bash
# Mepụta ihe nzuzo anọ ahụ niile n’otu oge:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!CAUTION]
> Etinyela faịlụ `.env` nwere ezi ihe nzuzo na njikwa ụdị. `.gitignore` ewepụlarị `.env`, mana nyochaa ya tupu ịkwanye.

---

## 2. Nchekwa & Ebe Nchekwa Data

OmniRoute na-eji **SQLite** (site na `better-sqlite3`) maka nchekwa data niile na-adịgide adịgide. Mgbanwe ndị a na-achịkwa ebe data dị, izochi ya, na usoro ndụ ya.

| Mgbanwe                                    | Ndabara                       | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Akwụkwọ ndekọ mgbọrọgwụ maka DB SQLite, ndabere, na faịlụ data. Gbanwee ya maka mpịakọta Docker ma ọ bụ ụzọ ahaziri iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Ụzọ mgbapụ maka ihe nche DATA_DIR nke ule/ntụle (#10428). A na-atụgharị ule na nyocha eval/print nke Node (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, gụnyere ụdị `--eval=`/`--print=`) ndị na-enweghị `DATA_DIR` gaa na akwụkwọ ndekọ nwa oge a ga-atụfu, ka ha ghara imepe ebe nchekwa data nke onye na-ahụ maka sistemụ n'ezie; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji họrọ iji ezigbo akwụkwọ ndekọ ahụ ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA nke artifact na-arụ ọrụ. `npm run build:release` na-akanye ya; enwere ike itinye ya n'ime containers ndị a na-eziga na-enweghị akara `dist/BUILD_SHA`. A na-egosi ya dịka `system.buildSha` na `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ref nke ọnụ ụzọ nyocha mmalite pack-artifact ji atụnyere build SHA (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(edoghị)_                    | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji kwe ka a kwakọba build nke SHA ya na-anọghị n'ahịrị mwepụta, ma dekọọ ya dịka canary e mere ụma kama ime ka ọnụ ụzọ nyocha ahụ daa (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(edoghị)_                    | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | Igodo API maka nyocha anwụrụ ọkụ nke canary-deploy, nke a na-eziga dịka `Authorization: Bearer` na `/v1/chat/completions`. Naanị script mbugharị na-eji ya (#10429), sava anaghị eji ya ma ọlị. O nweghị njikọ na mgbanwe `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` nke ngwa nyocha anwụrụ ọkụ CLI a na-ahọrọ iji (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` n'ime `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) — lee [Njikọ CLI → Nnyocha anwụrụ ọkụ n'ezie](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Akara usoro build (#10060): `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` na-edo ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`, ndị ọrụ build niile e mepụtara na-eketakwa ya, ka oyi akwa DB weghachite stub na-adịghị eme ihe kama ibunye addon better-sqlite3 nke native (nke na-akwụsị onye ọrụ ahụ mgbe ọ na-apụ). Edola ya mgbe ọ bụla maka sava na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                           | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji mafere nyocha prebuild nke native-dependency nhọrọ maka osisi vendored pụrụ iche. Nke a anaghị eme ka dependency na-efu nwee ike ịrụ build; jiri ya naanị mgbe a na-enye native dependencies site n'ụzọ ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(edoghị)_                    | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **Aha ọzọ ndabere** maka `DATA_DIR`, nke a na-enyocha naanị mgbe edoghị `DATA_DIR`. A na-eji ya achọta cache session thread-sticky nke PromptQL executor dị na diski (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); ọ bụrụ na edoghị mgbanwe abụọ ahụ, cache ahụ ga-anọ naanị na ebe nchekwa (agaghị echekwa ya n'etiti mmalitegharị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Akwụkwọ ndekọ nke **runtime plugin scanner** na-agụ — nakwa mgbọrọgwụ ebe plugin manager na-etinye ihe — nke na-anọchi ndabara sitere na home (#11827). Tụọ ya aka n'osisi plugin e ji bind mount n'ime Docker/K8s kama ịkwaga HOME naanị iji gbanwee ebe ụzọ nyocha dị (HOME na-achịkwa omume ndị ọzọ niile dabere na home kwa). Mgbe edoghị ya = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, ma ọ bụ `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` mgbe process ahụ anaghị ebupụ home ọ bụla — nke a bụ enweghị nchọpụta na-enweghị ọkwa nke mgbanwe a na-ewepụ. A na-edekọ akwụkwọ ndekọ edoziworo otu ugboro mgbe mmalite dịka `scanner.dir_resolved`, tinyere input nke meriri. Ọ bụ naanị n'akụkụ sava: plugins iwu CLI na-edobe `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` nke ha (ngalaba 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _(gbọmgbọm = agbanyụrụ)_      | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | Igodo AES maka izochi nchekwa data SQLite niile mgbe echekwara ya. Jiri `openssl rand -hex 32` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                          | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Akara ụdị maka igodo izochi ozi. Welie ya mgbe a na-atụgharị igodo ka e nwee ike ịtọghe nkwado ndabere ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, ọ na-amafere nkwado ndabere faịlụ SQLite nke a na-eme kwa oge/tupu ide ihe (ichekwa/ihichapụ ọnụahịa models.dev, ide ntọala). Nkwado ndabere aka na nke tupu iweghachi ka na-arụ ọrụ. Ọ **naghị** agbanyụ snapshot nchekwa na-adịgide adịgide nke onye na-eme mbugharị na-amanye, ma ọ bụ ihe mgbochi nnukwu mbugharị maka DB na-adịgide adịgide dịbu. A na-amachi nkwado ndabere ndị na-abụghị nke aka ka ha ghara ịkarị otu ugboro n'ime nkeji 60. **Ntọala → Nchekwa** dị na dashboard nwere ike ịgbanyụ nkwado ndabere akpaaka a na-eme kwa oge n'onwe ya.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(edobeghị)_                  | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Aha nnọchi ochie** maka `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. A na-anabata ya dịka nhọrọ ndabere mgbe mgbanwe bụ isi adịghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(edobeghị)_                  | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | A na-anabata **aha nnọchi ochie** (ụdị e ji Base64 koodu) dịka nhọrọ ndabere. A na-atọghe koodu ya na-akpaghị aka tupu e jiri ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DB_HEALTHCHECK_INTERVAL_MS`     | _(edobeghị)_                  | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Gbanwee oge dị n'etiti nyocha ahụike SQLite nke a na-eme kwa oge (ms). Mgbe edobeghị ya, a na-enweta ụkpụrụ ndabara site na `NODE_ENV`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | _(ewepụla)_                   | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | **Ewepụla ya.** `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` dị ndụ nke a na-eme kwa oge nwere ike ime ka njikọ wal-index a na-ekekọrịta ghara ịdị irè ma mee ka usoro ahụ daa site na SIGBUS (#13973), ya mere onye nhazi oge ahụ adịghịzi. Mgbanwe a anaghịzi arụ ọrụ: uru dị mma na-edekọ ịdọ aka ná ntị otu ugboro na a kwụsịla iji ya, ebe `0` ma ọ bụ uru edobeghị na-anọ jụụ. A na-elekọta WAL site na checkpoint PASSIVE (n'okpuru), ebe checkpoint mgbe a na-emechi usoro na-ebipụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_PASSIVE_INTERVAL_MS`        | `300000` (nkeji 5)            | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Gbanwee oge dị n'etiti `wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)` ndị a na-eme ugboro ugboro (ms). Ọ na-eme ka frame WAL ndị na-echere dị obere, ka checkpoint wee na-adị ngwa ma faịlụ WAL ghara ịgafe oke n'etiti mbepụ ndị a na-eme mgbe usoro na-emechi. `0` na-agbanyụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_GUARD_MAX_MB`               | `256`                         | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Mgbe tick PASSIVE chọpụtara na faịlụ WAL karịrị nha a, mee `wal_checkpoint(RESTART)` ka WAL malitegharịa n'edegharịghị wal-index e tinyere na ebe nchekwa. E wepụla checkpoint ụdị truncate ndị dị ndụ (lee ahịrị `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji kwụsị usoro ahụ mgbe nrụgide akụ dị oke njọ nọgidere ruo `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS`, ka onye nlekọta (systemd `Restart=always`, iwu mbugharị Docker) nwee ike iweghachi usoro dị ọcha kama ịnọgide na-enye 503 ruo mgbe ebighị ebi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (nkeji 2)            | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Ogologo oge nrụgide dị oke njọ ga-anọgide tupu nkwụsị maka ịmalitegharị onwe ya amalite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_PSI_DISABLED`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressurePolicy.ts`            | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji leghara `/proc/pressure/memory` (`psi_some` / `psi_full`) nke host dum anya mgbe agbata obi na-eme mkpọtụ na-akpalite 503 ebe usoro a dị mma (#13124). Ihe nche V8 heap na cgroup ka na-arụ ọrụ. PSI na-akwụsịkwa ịgụ dịka ihe akaebe na kernel na-amachi ọsọ maka `cgroup_high`: mkpokọta cgroup karịrị `memory.high` ga-aghọzi nke dị oke njọ naanị mgbe uru `high`/`max` nke `memory.events` mụbara. Ọ bụrụ na `memory.events` adịghị, a na-edobe oke raw ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLJS_WASM_PATH`                | _(nchọpụta akpaaka)_          | `src/lib/db/adapters/sqljsAdapter.ts`                 | Ụzọ doro anya (zuru ezu ma ọ bụ dabere na cwd) gaa na `sql-wasm.wasm` mgbe a na-eji adapter ndabere WASM nke `sql.js`. Mgbe edobeghị ya, a na-achọpụta ya na-akpaghị aka site na ndabere package na nhazi ndị nwere ike ịbụ nhọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`           | `30`                          | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Ọnụọgụ ụbọchị a na-edobe checkpoint, faịlụ input/output/error e zoro aka na ha, na ahịrị nke ọrụ Batch API ruru ngwụcha (completed/failed/cancelled/expired) tupu mkpocha akpaaka ehichapụ ha. Ọ na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ naanị mgbe agbanyere `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`; ọ dabara na oge OpenAI n'onwe ya ji edobe output Batch API. Ọ naghị emetụta route `DELETE /api/v1/batches/delete-completed` nke onye njikwa na-akpalite, nke na-anọgide na-enweghị ọnọdụ ọ bụla (enweghị nzacha afọ) dịka e siri hazie ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`      | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, kwe ka ọrụ nhicha akpaaka hichapụ ọrụ Batch API ndị ruru ọnọdụ ikpeazụ (na checkpoints ha) gafere `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`, ma kpochapụ ọdịnaya BLOB nke faịlụ ebulitere ndị gafere `expires_at` nke ha. Ọ na-agbanyụ na ndabara: nrụnye niile dị ugbu a ga-edobe data a otu ọ dị na mbụ ruo mgbe onye nchịkwa họrọ ịgbanye ya. Ọ bụkwa akara atụmatụ enwere ike idezi na dashboard — lee `docs/reference/FEATURE_FLAGS.md` → Runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`            | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`, `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji mafere nyocha ahụike DB kpamkpam mgbe usoro na-amalite. Ọ bara uru maka ọrụ na-adị obere oge na ule njikọta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORCE_DB_HEALTHCHECK`           | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji manye loop nyocha ahụike DB ka ọ gbanye, ọbụna mgbe a gaara amafere ya n'ọnọdụ nkịtị (dịka ọmụmaatụ, ọrụ na-adị obere oge).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_POSTINSTALL`               | `0`                           | `scripts/postinstall.mjs`                             | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji mafere nkwadebe native-runtime n'oge `npm install`. Ọ bara uru na nrụnye CI/headless ebe ewurula sqlite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_MIGRATIONS_DIR`                 | _(chọpụta na-akpaghị aka)_    | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Dochie ndekọ nke migration runner na-enyocha. Ọ bara uru mgbe a na-ebunye migrations agbakọtara ọnụ n'ime build ahaziri iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_EXTRA_MIGRATIONS_DIRS`          | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner/extraDirs.ts`             | Ndekọ migration ndị ọzọ dị ka ndenye `namespace=dir` ndị e ji ihe nkewa path nke platform kewaa (dịka ọmụmaatụ `ee=/opt/app/enterprise/db/migrations`). A na-edekọ faịlụ ndị a hụrụ ebe ahụ dị ka `<namespace>-<number>`, ya mere nkesa nke na-ebunye migrations nke ya agaghị adakọrịta na oghere ọnụọgụ nke upstream. Ndenye arụghị nke ọma, namespace na-ezighi ezi, ma ọ bụ ndekọ na-adịghị adị ga-ebute njehie mgbe usoro na-amalite kama ịmafe schema ahụ na nzuzo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_PENDING_MIGRATIONS`         | `50`                          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Oke nchekwa maka ọtụtụ migrations na-echere (#3416). Mmalite usoro ga-akwụsị ma ọ bụrụ na migrations na-echere karịrị ọnụọgụ a na DB dị ugbu a (nke a na-echebe megide tracking table e kpochapụrụ). Bulie ya iji weghachite backup ochie; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ nyocha ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTALL_UPGRADE_WORKDIR`        | _(`<repo>/.install-upgrade`)_ | `scripts/check/check-install-upgrade.mjs`             | Ndekọ ọrụ maka ọnụ ụzọ ntọhapụ `check:install-upgrade`. Ọ chọrọ ihe dịka 12 GB (osisi nrụnye abụọ nke ọ bụla dị ihe dịka 3 GB tinyere tarball), ya mere, ọ gaghị arụ ọrụ na tmpfs pere mpe — na self-hosted runner, `/tmp` bụ tmpfs 12 GB nke RAM na-akwado, ọnụ ụzọ ahụ mekwara ka ohere ya gwụ, nke mere ka package ahụ bepụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS`        | _(ndabara dị na code)_        | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Ogologo oge (ms) n'etiti flush maka onye na-ede spend/cost n'ìgwè. Uru dị ala na-ebelata ijikọ write; uru dị elu na-ebelata esemokwu DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`          | _(ndabara dị na code)_        | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Ọnụọgụ ndenye spend kachasị enwere ike idowe na buffer tupu amanye flush. Bulie ya na deployment nwere QPS dị elu; belata ya mgbe oke memory dị mkpa karịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_FETCH_DEBUG`              | _(edoghị)_                    | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                        | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` iji wepụta ndekọ debug `[ProxyFetch]` n'ụzọ relay Vercel. Ọ na-agbanyụ na ndabara iji zere ikpughe ntụnye routing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_BINARY_PATH`         | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/sync.ts`        | Path zuru oke nke binary proxy core dị n'ógbè ahụ (faịlụ aha ya bụ `sing-box`) nke na-enyocha nhazi core emepụtara tupu ọ dochie faịlụ a nabatara. Mgbe edoghị ya, ọ na-ede naanị `<path>.generated` n'akụkụ faịlụ a nabatara. Naanị maka gburugburu host: anaghị echekwa ya na database ma ọ bụ nabata ya site na management API, n'ihi na a na-eme binary ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_RELOAD_COMMAND`      | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/reload.ts`      | Otu proxy core dị n'ógbè ahụ si eme reload mgbe edochiri config nke enyocharala: n'usoro argument JSON a na-agba na-enweghị shell, ma ọ bụ kpọmkwem `external` mgbe onye nlekọta nọ n'èzí na-etinye faịlụ ahụ. Anaghị echekwa ya na database ma ọ bụ kpughee ya site na API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                    | `false`                       | `src/lib/proxyLogger.ts`                              | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` ma ọ bụ `"1"` iji tinye IP ndị client/egress na prefix account n'ahịrị process-log `[ProxyEgress]` nwere nkọwa zuru ezu. A na-edobe ya ka ọ bụrụ OFF na ndabara ka process log ghara ikpughe IP ma ọ bụ prefix account.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(edoghị ya)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji bipụta nchọpụta oge arịrịọ ọ bụla (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) site na iwu oke CLI gaa na stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_PATH`               | _(akpaghị aka)_               | `scripts/dev/healthcheck.mjs`                         | Ụzọ akọwapụtara nke nyocha ahụike konteena na-enyocha. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, nyocha ahụ ga-ewepụta ya site na `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`; ịtọ ya na-eme ka e jiri endpoint nlekota miri emi ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(edoghị ya)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ uru ọ bụla na-abụghị efu iji wepụta nchọpụta `[omniroute completion]` site na ụzọ cache mmecha shell CLI (gụ/mee ka ọ dị ọhụrụ/dee). Agbanyụghị ya na ndabara — cache ndị ahụ na-ada n'ekwughị ihe ọ bụla ka cache na-efu ma ọ bụ nke mebiri emebi ghara imebi mmecha site na tab.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (24h)              | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Oge kachasị elu maka ịnwale ihe batch ọ bụla ọzọ (ms). A na-akara ihe ndị gafere oge a dị ka ndị dara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Oge nkwụsị ntọala (ms) maka exponential backoff mgbe a na-anwale ihe batch ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (1h)                | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Oke kachasị (ms) maka exponential backoff n'etiti nnwale ọzọ nke ihe batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Ọnụọgụ batch kachasị elu a ga-ahazi n'otu oge. Welie ya iji mụbaa throughput; debe ya ala iji zere oke arịrịọ buru ibu n'otu oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Tupu ịgbanwe database na-adịgide adịgide dịbu, onye na-agba migration na-ebipụta snapshot zuru ezu,
> nke adreesị ya dabere na ọdịnaya, n'okpuru `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Mbipụta chọrọ filesystem
> nke na-akwado hard link ndị nọ n'otu filesystem, ndị anaghị edegharị faịlụ dịbu, tinyere sync faịlụ na-adịgide adịgide. Host POSIX chọkwara
> sync directory; na Windows, Node nwere ike ịjụ handle directory, ya mere OmniRoute na-eme flush
> faịlụ e bipụtara ma na-ewere sync nke ntinye directory dị ka mbọ kacha mma.
> Ọ bụrụ na `DATA_DIR` e mountara enweghị ike inye nkwa ndị ahụ, mmalite ga-akwụsị n'ụzọ nchebe tupu etinye
> migration. Bugharịa `DATA_DIR` gaa na volume nwere ikike ndị ahụ; ejila
> `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` gafere nchekwa migration.

### Ọnọdụ ojiji

| Ọnọdụ ojiji                  | Nhazi                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Mmepe mpaghara**           | Hapụ ndabara niile. DB dị na `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.                              |
| **Docker**                   | `DATA_DIR=/data` + mount volume na `/data`.                                            |
| **Ezoro ezo mgbe echekwara** | Tọọ `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + debe nkwado ndabere nke key ahụ! Itufu ya = itufu data. |
| **CI/Nnwale**                | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` — nke nwa oge, encryption adịghị mkpa.                  |

---

## 3. Netwọkụ & Ọdụmbata

| Mgbanwe                                     | Ndabara                                              | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                                              | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Ọdụmbata bụ isi maka Dashboard UI na ebe njedebe API (ọnọdụ otu ọdụmbata).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(oghere = mgbọrọgwụ)_                               | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | Ụzọ nta URL maka ijere OmniRoute ozi n'azụ proxy ntụgharị (na-ahazi `basePath` nke Next.js; ntụgharị auth na-eburu basePath n'uche). Dịka ọmụmaatụ, `/omniroute`. Na Docker, a na-etinye uru ahụ mgbe a na-eme `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); onyonyo mgbọrọgwụ ndị e wuru tupu oge eruo nwere ike itinye uru runtime dị iche otu ugboro mgbe container malitere tupu Next.js ebido. Hazie `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` ka ọ bụrụ isi mmalite ọha nke gụnyere otu ụzọ nta ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(oghere = mgbọrọgwụ)_                               | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Oyiri `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` nke ihe nchọgharị nwere ike ịhụ, nke a na-etinye n'ime ya n'oge owuwu ka ngosipụta ebe njedebe dashboard gosi `https://host/omniroute/v1` kama `https://host/v1`. Ọ na-alaghachi na `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` mgbe a na-ahazighị ya. Wughachi ya mgbe ị gbanwechara ya (`basePath` nke Next bụ nke oge owuwu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(ahazighị = enweghị ike itinye ya na frame ma ọlị)_ | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Nhọrọ iji kwado itinye ibe HTML n'ime iframe. Mgbe ahazighị ya, ụzọ ọ bụla na-ebunye `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Hazie ya ka ọ bụrụ `vscode` iji jiri `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` jee ozi ibe ndị ahụ (dashboard, nbanye, akwụkwọ, ibe mbata), na-enweghị `X-Frame-Options`, ka VS Code Simple Browser nwee ike igosipụta ha (ọnọdụ `dashboardOpen: "editor"` nke OmniCopilot). Elu API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, aha nnọchi ndị dị n'ogo mgbọrọgwụ) na-edobe isiokwu nche siri ike n'ụzọ ọ bụla. Naanị `vscode` ka a na-amata — `1`/`true` anaghị akwado ya. Oge owuwu: wughachi mgbe ị gbanwechara ya (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` maka onyonyo; ịhazi ya na nrụnye e wuru tupu oge eruo enweghị mmetụta).                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(ahazighị)_                                         | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Mgbe ahaziri ya, ọ na-enye proxy API `/v1/*` n'ọdụmbata a dị iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                                            | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Adreesị njikọta maka ọdụmbata API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(ahazighị)_                                         | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Mgbe ahaziri ya, ọ na-enye Dashboard UI n'ọdụmbata a dị iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(agbanyụrụ)_                                    | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Na-amachi njikọ nkata ndị na-aga n'otu oge; arịrịọ gafere oke ahụ na-enweta `503` tinyere `Retry-After`. Nọmba zuru ezu dị mma na-akwado ihe nche a; enweghị nhazi/`0` na-agbanyụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(ahazighị)_                                         | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | ID kwụsiri ike na pụrụ iche maka ọnụ ụzọ a mgbe a na-ejikọ instance OmniRoute n'usoro. Na-akwado nyocha loop nke peer na-abata. Mkpụrụedemede anabatara: mkpụrụedemede, ọnụọgụ, `.`, `_`, `:`, na `-`; karịa mkpụrụedemede 64 agaghị ekwe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(edoghị ya)_                                        | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | URL ntọala OmniRoute ndị e ji rikoma kewaa nke nwere ike ịnata `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`. Naanị URL upstream ndị etinyere kpọmkwem na ndepụta ikike ga-enweta metadata peer; a naghị emetụ ndị na-eweta ọrụ ndị ọzọ niile aka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_MAX_HOPS`                   | `4`                                                  | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Ọnụọgụ kachasị elu nke instance OmniRoute ndị a gara na mbụ nke a na-anabata na arịrịọ ejikọtara ọnụ (`1`-`32`). Instance ugboro ugboro ma ọ bụ mmefu oke njem niile ga-eweghachi HTTP `508 Loop Detected`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT`                       | `20130`                                              | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port e bipụtara n'akụkụ host maka Dashboard na ọnọdụ production Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROD_API_PORT`                             | `20131`                                              | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port e bipụtara n'akụkụ host maka API na ọnọdụ production Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PORT`                            | _(edoghị ya)_                                        | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Ọ na-ebute ụzọ karịa `PORT` mgbe a na-agba ya n'ime Electron ma ọ bụ wrapper ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `LIVE_WS_PORT`                              | `20129`                                              | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Port maka sava nlekota ozugbo nke WebSocket.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `LIVE_WS_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                          | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Adreesị bind maka sava WebSocket ozugbo. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0.0.0.0` iji kpughee ya na LAN (haziekwa `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`). Njikọ na-eji kuki session dashboard ma ọ bụ igodo Bearer API nwere scope njikwa (`manage`/`admin`) eme nkwenye njirimara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                   | _(edoghị ya)_                                        | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Origin ndị ọzọ e ji rikoma kewaa nke enyere ohere imepe WebSocket ozugbo. A na-enye origin dashboard loopback ohere na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_HOSTS`                     | _(edoghị ya)_                                        | `src/server/ws/liveServerAllowList.ts`                                   | Aha host ndị ọzọ e ji rikoma kewaa nke enyere ohere maka origin WebSocket ozugbo. N'adịghị ka `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (URL origin zuru ezu), ọ na-adakọ naanị akụkụ host — nke bara uru maka nhazi LAN/Tailscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_WS_PUBLIC_URL`            | _(edoghị ya)_                                        | `src/hooks/useLiveDashboard.ts`                                          | URL ọha maka WebSocket dashboard ozugbo (n'akụkụ ihe nchọgharị). Tọọ ya mgbe ị na-eji reverse proxy ma ọ bụ Cloudflare Tunnel debe sava WS n'ihu (dịka `wss://ws.my-ai.com/live-ws`); ihe nchọgharị ga-ejikọ ebe ahụ kama `ws://hostname:20132`. A na-ejikwa akụkụ pathname dị ka ụzọ nkwalite WebSocket (ndabara: `/live-ws`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_LIVE_WS`                  | `true`                                               | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts` na `scripts/start-ws-server.mjs`           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` ma ọ bụ `false` iji gbanyụọ sava WebSocket ozugbo (agbanyere ya na ndabara, ma kee ya na loopback). Mgbanwe CI/harness nke na-agbanyụọ skripti enyemaka WebSocket ozugbo kwụụrụ onwe ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE`                       | `30`                                                 | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/route.ts`                         | Oke ọsọ relay kwa-(token, IP), arịrịọ/nkeji. Ọ dị na ebe nchekwa, kwa instance. `0` ma ọ bụ uru na-adịghị mma na-agbanyụọ ọnụ ụzọ akụkụ IP (oke DB kwa-token ka na-emetụta).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
