| `NODE_ENV`                                  | `production`                                         | Next.js core                                                             | Na-achịkwa oke nkọwa ndekọ, caching, ikpughe nkọwa njehie, na nkwalite Next.js.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK`                   | `1` (Turbopack — ndabara nke koodu)                  | `package.json` / Next.js 16                                              | Turbopack bụ bundler ndabara maka `npm run dev` na `npm run build` (owuwu ji ọsọ ugboro 2-3, dịka benchmark gosiri). Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji laghachi na webpack na Windows, mgbe ị zutere enweghị ndakọrịta native binding / bundler-compat, **ma ọ bụ na kọmputa RAM ya pere mpe** — a maara n'akụkụ upstream na owuwu production Turbopack n'ahịrị ụdị Next.js a (16.2.x) na-eru ebe nchekwa dị elu nke ukwuu karịa webpack n'elu nnukwu module graphs (ndozi memory-eviction nke Turbopack na Next 16.3 akwụsibeghị ike); fallback webpack na-eru ọkwa dị ala nke ukwuu. Lee #6409. **Owuwu Docker image na-eji `0` (webpack) dịka ndabara**: `Dockerfile` na-ekwupụta `ARG OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=0` ka `docker build .` nkịtị (Railway na ndị nnabata one-click ndị ọzọ, yana ndị na-anabata sava nke ha n'oge ụfọdụ) ghara ịnata OOM-kill n'elu builder nwere oke ebe nchekwa; nyefee `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=1` iji Turbopack wuo image ahụ. Lee `docs/guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md` → Akụrụngwa oge owuwu. |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`             | _(edobeghị)_                                         | `src/lib/db/core.ts` / `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`                       | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji wụfee nlele ahụike iguzosi ike n'ezi ihe nke SQLite mgbe ọ na-amalite. Ọ bara uru maka mmalite dị ngwa n'elu nnukwu databases.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(edobeghị)_                                         | systemd (protocol sd_notify)                                             | systemd na-edobe ya mgbe process na-agba ọsọ n'okpuru service unit nwere njikọ sd_notify; OmniRoute na-agụ ya (lee `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) iji zipụ ọkwa READY/WATCHDOG. Onye ọrụ ekwesịghị idobe ya ma ọlị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(edobeghị)_                                         | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji gbanyụọ systemd sd_notify (Type=notify / WatchdogSec=) ọbụna mgbe ọ na-agba ọsọ n'okpuru systemd unit. Notifier ahụ anaghị eme ihe ọ bụla mgbe ọ nọghị na systemd n'agbanyeghị ọnọdụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL`          | `3600000`                                            | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts` | Ogologo oge (ms) n'etiti ọrụ nlele ahụike credential nke scheduler na-arụ n'azụ. Nke kacha nta: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                                             | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) maka ọnọdụ ahụike credential echekwara na cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                                              | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` ma ọ bụ `true` iji gbanyụọ nnwale njikọ provider nke a na-eme mgbe ụfọdụ n'azụ. A na-ewepụ search providers (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS dị na `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, dịka `tavily-search`) mgbe niile na sweep ahụ — "validation" ha bụ ezigbo upstream query a na-akwụ ụgwọ, ya mere a naghị enyocha ahụike ha site na timer (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DEEP_HEALTH_CHECK_ENABLED`                 | `0`                                                  | `src/app/api/monitoring/health/route.ts`                                 | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji kwe ka onye kpọrọ oku nke enyochala njirimara ya rịọ `/api/monitoring/health?deep=1`, nke na-ewere sample nke completions surface otu ugboro n'ime TTL ọ bụla. Ndị na-akpọ oku n'amaghị aha anaghị akpalite probe ahụ ma ọlị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                                            | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Adreesị bind maka sava dev/start nke Next.js. Ọ bụrụ na edobere ya, ọ ga-anọchi ndabara `0.0.0.0`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HOSTNAME`                                  | `127.0.0.1`                                          | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`                                    | Adreesị bind nke Playwright runner na-eji mgbe ọ na-ebido Next.js. Ọ na-eji `127.0.0.1` dịka ndabara maka nnwale hermetic. **Ejila ya maka `omniroute serve`** — jiri `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST` kama (POSIX shells na-edobe `HOSTNAME` n'onwe ha ka ọ bụrụ aha kọmputa; `.env` enweghị ike ịgbanwe ya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST`                     | `0.0.0.0`                                            | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                             | Adresị njikọ maka `omniroute serve`. Ọ na-egbochi ndakọrịta ya na mgbanwe `HOSTNAME` nke shei POSIX (bash/zsh na-edobe ya mgbe niile ka ọ bụrụ aha igwe). Ọ na-eji `0.0.0.0` ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya. (#6194)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

### Ụdị Ọdụ Oghere

```
┌────────────────────── Otu Ọdụ Oghere (ndabara) ────────────────────────────┐
│  PORT=20128                                                                 │
│  → Ogwe njikwa: http://localhost:20128                                      │
│  → API:          http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions                  │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌──────────────────────── Ọdụ Oghere Kewara Ekewa ───────────────────────────┐
│  DASHBOARD_PORT=20128                                                       │
│  API_PORT=20129                                                             │
│  API_HOST=0.0.0.0                                                           │
│  → Ogwe njikwa: http://localhost:20128                                      │
│  → API:          http://0.0.0.0:20129/v1/chat/completions                   │
│  Ojiji: Mee ka LAN nweta API ma gbochie Ogwe njikwa ka ọ nọrọ na localhost. │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌──────────────────────── Mmepụta Docker ────────────────────────────────────┐
│  PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT=443   PROD_API_PORT=8443                              │
│  → Ọ na-ejikọta ọdụ oghere konteena na ọdụ oghere host na docker-compose.prod.yml. │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Nchekwa & Nkwenye Njirimara

| Varebụl                                     | Ndabara                                                                                                | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MACHINE_ID_SALT`                           | `endpoint-proxy-salt`                                                                                  | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Salt a na-ejikọta na ihe njirimara ngwaike maka imepụta akara mkpịsịaka igwe. Gbanwee ya maka ntinye ọ bụla iji kewapụ ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SALT`                        | _(ahazighị = salt enweghị usoro maka ntinye ọ bụla nke echekwara na `<DATA_DIR>/cli-token-salt.json`)_ | `src/lib/machineToken.ts`                                | Salt HMAC maka iwepụta token nkwenye njirimara CLI mpaghara. Ịtọ uru a na-agbanwe token CLI niile dị n'igwe ahụ ma na-ebute salt echekwara ụzọ mgbe niile. Lee `docs/security/CLI_TOKEN.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE`                        | `false`                                                                                                | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Na-etinye ọkọlọtọ `Secure` na kuki nnọkọ. **Ọ ga-abụrịrị `true`** mgbe a na-arụ ọrụ n'azụ HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `REQUIRE_API_KEY`                           | `false`                                                                                                | Middleware API                                           | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, arịrịọ proxy `/v1/*` niile ga-etinyerịrị igodo API ziri ezi. **Ọkọlọtọ a anaghị achịkwa `GET /v1/models`**, nke na-agbaso ọnọdụ nbanye dashboard (`requireAuthForModels`) kama — ya mere, `401` sitere na `/v1/models` APỤTAGHỊ na e chebere inference. Lee `docs/security/INFERENCE_AUTH_POSTURE.md` (#13695).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL`                      | `false`                                                                                                | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`         | Na-enye ohere igosi uru igodo API zuru ezu na UI Dashboard. Enwere ike ịhazi ya site na Feature Flags nke Dashboard; ọ bụ ihe ize ndụ nchekwa n'instans ndị ọtụtụ mmadụ na-eji ọnụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NO_LOG_API_KEY_IDS`                        | _(efu)_                                                                                                | `src/lib/compliance/index.ts`                            | ID igodo API ndị e ji rikoma kewaa nke na-agafe ndekọ arịrịọ (nrubeisi GDPR).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `DEFAULT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_DAY`                | _(ahazighị = enweghị oke)_                                                                             | `src/shared/utils/apiKeyPolicy.ts`                       | Atụmatụ arịrịọ ndabere kwa ụbọchị a na-etinye na igodo API ndị kọlụm `rate_limits` ha bụ null. Ọ bụrụ na ahazighị ya ma ọ bụ na ọ tọgbọ chakoo: enweghị oke ndabara (#2289, #11017). `0` bụkwa otu ihe ahụ (enweghị oke). Nọmba mgbakọ dị mma N na-eme ka N/ụbọchị, 5N/izu, 20N/ọnwa rụọ ọrụ. Uru na-abụghị efu nke ahazighị nke ọma na-alaghachi na windo ochie nke 1000/ụbọchị, 5000/izu, 20000/ọnwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES`                       | `10485760` (10 MB)                                                                                     | `src/shared/middleware/bodySizeGuard.ts`                 | Ogo kacha ukwuu a na-anabata maka body arịrịọ. Ọ na-ajụ payload ndị gafere oke a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`           | `262144` (256 KB)                                                                                      | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Body arịrịọ n'ezie ndị ruru ma ọ bụ gafere oke a na-ewere lease nnabata heavyweight nke usoro mpaghara n'ụzọ atomic tupu parsing JSON (ụzọ BYTE, gụnyere `POST /v1/responses`). Otu ụzọ mgbapụ healthy-headroom nke [#10437](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/pull/10437) dị ka nke nwere nhazi dị arọ; `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / [#10110](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10110) ka na-amachi ya ka [#7849](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/7849) ghara ịlaghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_BODY_BYTES`        | `52428800` (50 MB)                                                                                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Oke kachasị siri ike nke chat-route, nke a na-amanye megide byte ndị a gụrụ n'oge nnabata nwere oke, gụnyere arịrịọ ndị `Content-Length` ha na-efu, na-ezighi ezi, ma ọ bụ na-abụghị eziokwu; ihe karịrị oke na-enweta `413`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT`        | _(edobeghị — enweghị oke dabere n'ọnụọgụ arịrịọ)_                                                      | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | **#503-fanout:** oke ochie a dabere na ỌNỤỌGỤ arịrịọ na-arụ ọrụ naanị mgbe edobere ya n'ụzọ doro anya. Mgbe edobeghị ya (ndabara), a na-achịkwa nnabata chat dị arọ kama site na `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` — mmefu BYTE a na-emepụta na-akpaghị aka, nke a na-agbakọ site n'oke ebe nchekwa n'ezie nke usoro ahụ n'ime **otu process** (otu V8 heap). Arịrịọ `/v1/responses` abụọ jikọtara oge, nke ọ bụla nwere ihe dịka token 750k, na-eme ka heap dị ihe dịka 12 Gi kwụsị (#7849) — nke a bụ ịdọ aka ná ntị gbasara **mmefu ebe nchekwa**, ọ bụghị oke ngwaahịa siri ike nke 2. Process dị mma (heap dị n'okpuru shed ratio) NWERE IKE ịnabata ọtụtụ `/v1/responses` ogologo n'otu oge site na `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM`. Ọtụtụ ndị ahịa SSE ogologo (40–50) metụtara heap + `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / #10110, ọ bụghị “oke kachasị 2”. Ibulite nke a n'amaghị ama iji “jiri host ahụ” na-eweghachite #7849. Mụbaa **heap** site na **`DATA_DIR` N nọọrọ onwe ha** (#11024); ejila `replicas>1` n'otu faịlụ SQLite. |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES`         | _(a na-emepụta na-akpaghị aka)_                                                                        | `src/shared/middleware/admissionBudget.ts`               | **#503-fanout:** ngbanwe maka mmefu byte nnabata a na-emepụta na-akpaghị aka (25% nke oke ebe nchekwa V8/cgroup nke kacha nta, kewara site na mmụba nwa oge 8x). A na-amachi uru ndị emepụtara na ndị edobere kpọmkwem n'etiti 8 MiB–2 GiB. Body buru ibu karịa mmefu dị irè na-ada ozugbo na `413 body_exceeds_budget`; esemokwu n'etiti body ndị enwere ike ijere ozi n'otu n'otu ka nwere ike ịnwale ọzọ site na `503`. Ndị ahịa SSE ogologo 40–50 na-arụkọ ọrụ n'otu oge metụtara mmefu a + heap, ọ bụghị “oke kachasị 2” siri ike. Gụọ `chatAdmission.maxInflightBytes` / `budgetSource` / `pressureSeverity` na `/api/monitoring/health` tupu imegharị nhazi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEAP_SHED_RATIO`  | `0.75`                                                                                                 | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Shed ratio nke nrụgide heap (`heapUsed / heap_size_limit`) maka nnabata BYTE na STRUCTURE dị arọ (#10183, #10268, #10437). A na-ewepụ arịrịọ dị arọ nke na-arụkọ ọrụ n'otu oge ma gafee `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` site na `503` a pụrụ ịnwale ọzọ naanị mgbe heap DỊKWA na ratio a ma ọ bụ karịa ya; na heap dị mma, a na-anabata ya kama site na healthy-headroom.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM` | `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` (ndabara `1`)                                                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ikike mgbakwunye nwere oke maka ụzọ ngwa ngwa nke healthy-heap (#10437) na **ma** STRUCTURE **ma** BYTE (`admitChatRequest`, gụnyere body ≥ `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`). Enweghị oke a, arịrịọ ọ bụla ji ọrụ n'aka mana heap ya dị mma na-agafe nnabata n'enweghị oke elu. Ozugbo lease ndị na-arụkọ ọrụ n'otu oge ruru ọnụ ọgụgụ a site n'ụzọ healthy-heap, arịrịọ ndị ọzọ ji ọrụ n'aka na-adaba n'otu ụzọ echere nwere oke/wepụ arịrịọ ahụ a na-eji mgbe enwere ezigbo nrụgide heap. `0` na-agbanyụ ngafe ahụ kpamkpam.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_MESSAGE_COUNT`        | `200`                                                                                                  | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ọnụọgụ ozi nke na-ekewa arịrịọ chat dị ka nke dị arọ ọbụna mgbe body ya dị n'okpuru oke byte.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_TOOL_COUNT`           | `64`                                                                                                   | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ọnụọgụ tool nke na-ekewa arịrịọ chat dị ka nke dị arọ ọbụna mgbe body ya dị n'okpuru oke byte.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_ESTIMATED_TOKENS`     | `32000`                                                                                                | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Atụmatụ token dabere n'ogo string nke ejiri akpachapụ anya, nke na-ekewa arịrịọ dị ka nke dị arọ; nke a bụ ihe nnọchi anya ọnụ ahịa nnabata, ọ bụghị tokenization nke provider ji akwụ ụgwọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_MESSAGES`          | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                                                                                        | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Oke akụkọ chat nhọrọ nke a ga-agbanye n'onwe ya. Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara: ọnụọgụ ozi bụ amụma deployment, ọ bụghị njirimara zuru ụwa ọnụ nke arịrịọ, yana ịmachi ya ebe a na-eji `413` ikpeazụ jụ mkparịta ụka tupu pipeline compression enwee ike ime ka e nwee ike ijere ha ozi. `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` na heap-pressure shed na-amachi uto heap. Debe uru dị mma na deployment ndị ebe nchekwa ha pere mpe ma chọọ oke elu siri ike; ihe karịrị oke ga-enwetazị `413` ahaziri nke na-achọ compact.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_NONSTREAMING_RESPONSE_BYTES` | `67108864` (64 MB)                                                                                     | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/nonStreamingResponseBody.ts` | Oke siri ike maka nzaghachi upstream na-abụghị streaming nke a na-echekwa n'uju n'ime ebe nchekwa. Mgbe ọ gafere nke a, a na-akagbu upstream reader ma arịrịọ ahụ na-ada ngwa ngwa kama ikwe ka string na-enweghị oke too ruo mgbe heap gwụsịrị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARDING_HEADER_BUDGET_BYTES`  | `768`                                                                                                  | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/responseHeaders.ts`          | Oke kachasị nke baitị wire a na-ebuga site na nkụnyeisi nzaghachi upstream. Mgbe a gafere oke a, a na-ewepụ nkụnyeisi ndị nwere mkpa dị ala (dịka, `x-codex-*`, `x-oai-request-id`) ka e wee nọrọ n'ime oke nkụnyeisi reverse-proxy ndị a na-ejikarị. Tọọ ya elu ka ebuga metadata upstream karịa, ma nke a ga-eme ka nha nkụnyeisi nzaghachi bawanye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CORS_ORIGIN`                               | _(edoghị)_                                                                                             | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Ndepụta nkwenye CORS nke otu origin nke ochie. Maka ntinye ọhụrụ, họrọ `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`. CORS bụ naanị maka ndị ahịa API ihe nchọgharị si na origin ọzọ; ide ihe na dashboard nke e ji njirimara banye na-eji arịrịọ same-origin tinyere nchedo CSRF ejikọtara na session kama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                      | _(edoghị)_                                                                                             | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Ndepụta nkwenye CORS nke e ji rikoma kewaa. A naghị eziga wildcard ọ bụla belụsọ ma ahazi `CORS_ALLOW_ALL=true` n'ụzọ doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CORS_ALLOW_ALL`                            | `false`                                                                                                | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Ụzọ mgbapụ maka mmepe naanị, nke na-emegharị `Origin` ihe nchọgharị ọ bụla. Emela ka ọ rụọ ọrụ na ntinye ndị a na-ekekọrịta ma ọ bụ nke production.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED`               | `true`                                                                                                 | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Aha nnọchi nke ochie. A na-agụ toggle dashboard ya (override DB) tupu uru environment a; `false`, `0`, `no`, ma ọ bụ `off` n'ime nke ọ bụla na-agbanyụ nyocha host dịka `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` si eme, ọbụlagodi mgbe echekwara toggle dashboard nke flag ahụ ka ọ gbanyụọ; uru ndị ọzọ na-ahapụ guard ahụ n'aka flag abụọ dị n'okpuru.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`     | `false`                                                                                                | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Na-agbanyụ nyocha host nke guard URL outbound, gụnyere mgbochi cloud-metadata (169.254.0.0/16 niile, metadata.google.internal), n'ụzọ provider ndị na-agbaso ọnọdụ guard: nkwado URL, nchọpụta model, base URL nke provider-node (gụnyere remote rerank nodes), na ule proxy-fallback, tinyere ụzọ outbound ndị ọzọ metụtara provider dịka iweta image/media dị remote. Ọ na-enyekwa ohere maka ebe webhook private na-aga, ewezuga endpoint cloud-metadata/link-local, ndị na-anọgide na-egbochi ọbụlagodi mgbe flag a dị ọkụ. Arịrịọ chat ndị e zigara site na `BaseExecutor.execute()` na-edobe nyocha cloud-metadata ha. Provider local na-arụ ọrụ na ndabara: `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` (ndabara `true`) enyelarị URL local na LAN ohere n'ụzọ nkwado, nchọpụta, na provider-node; ule proxy-fallback na ebe webhook private na-aga na-elele naanị flag a ma na-egbochi host local/LAN mgbe ọ gbanyụrụ. Ọ na-emeri `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS=false`. Uru echekwara site na toggle dashboard na-ebute ụzọ karịa variable a.         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS`       | `true`                                                                                                 | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Nye URL provider dị na adreesị local/private (127.0.0.1, localhost, LAN, private ranges) ohere maka nkwado provider, nchọpụta model, chat, na ụzọ outbound ndị ọzọ metụtara provider nke ọnọdụ guard na-achịkwa. **Ndabara `true`** (local-first): guard ahụ ga-egbochi endpoint cloud-metadata — 169.254.0.0/16 niile tinyere hostname metadata ndị amaara (metadata.google.internal, metadata.goog, Alibaba/Azure/Oracle IMDS, fd00:ec2::254). Tọọ `false` maka ọnọdụ public-only, nke na-egbochikwa host private na loopback. Guard ahụ na-enyocha hostname ma ọ bụ IP literal dịka e si dee ya, tupu nchọta DNS ọ bụla; na ọnọdụ public-only, wrapper safe-fetch na-emekwa resolve hostname ma jụ azịza private ọ bụla. `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` na-agbanyụ nyocha ndị a n'ụzọ ndị e depụtara n'ahịrị ya. Provider local arụnyere n'ime ya, dịka LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM, Llamafile, na Triton, na-amafere nyocha host niile maka nkwado key na chat. Uru echekwara site na toggle dashboard na-ebute ụzọ karịa variable a. (#5066)    |
| `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`               | `false`                                                                                                | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/audioProviderNodes.ts`           | Kwe ka route `/v1/audio/*` (transcriptions, speech, translations) jiri provider node dakọtara na OpenAI nke a na-host n'èzí localhost. Ọ na-agbanyụ na ndabara — iziga audio na host remote na-agbanwe njirimara egress, ya mere ọ ga-abụ mkpebi doro anya nke operator. A na-enye node loopback/private (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) ohere mgbe niile, nke a anaghịkwa emetụta ha. (#3963)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`              | `false`                                                                                                | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/rerankProviderNodes.ts`          | Kwe ka `POST /v1/rerank` (na usoro loopback rerank nke memory engine) jiri provider node dakọtara na OpenAI nke a na-host n'èzí localhost — igbe LAN ma ọ bụ peer Tailscale na-agba TEI, Infinity, vLLM, wdg. Ọ na-agbanyụ na ndabara — iziga na host remote na-agbanwe njirimara egress, ya mere ọ ga-abụ mkpebi doro anya nke operator. A na-enye node loopback (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) ohere mgbe niile, nke a anaghịkwa emetụta ha. Base URL nke node remote ga-agafekwa iwu URL outbound nke provider (`OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` / `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_PROVIDER_NODE_HOSTS`       | _(efu)_                                                                                                | `src/shared/network/localNodeHosts.ts`                   | Aha nnabata (nke rikoma ma ọ bụ oghere kewara) nke nhazi ụzọ provider-node na-ewere dị ka nke mpaghara — nke na-abụkarị aha ọrụ Docker/Compose nke reranker, TEI ma ọ bụ audio sidecar dị na netwọkụ Docker nke OmniRoute (`http://reranker:8080/v1`), ka node ahụ ghara ịchọ IP akpa e dere kpọmkwem. Ndị nnabata edepụtara tozuru maka `/v1/rerank`, `/v1/audio/*` na nyocha ahụike mpaghara na-enweghị ịgbanye `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` / `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` maka node ime anya ọ bụla, a na-akpọkwa ha ozugbo — ọ bụghị site na `HTTP(S)_PROXY` ma ọ bụ proxy e kekọtara na njikọ. Ha ka na-eziga API key nke njikọ ahụ. Naanị aha nwere otu label na aha `.internal`/`.local` ka a na-anabata; a na-eleghara FQDN ọha, IP literals na aha cloud-metadata anya ma nye ịdọ aka ná ntị. (#14635)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`     | `false`                                                                                                | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Mgbe agbanyere OIDC, gbanyụọ nbanye site na okwuntughe ka ndị ọrụ nwee ike ikwenye njirimara naanị site na OIDC Single Sign-On. A na-anabatakwa alias nkịtị `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`; Dashboard Feature Flag nwere otu key ahụ na-ebute ụzọ. (#10889)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`               | `false`                                                                                                | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Alias nkịtị nke `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` (#10889).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

### Ndepụta Nlele Maka Ime Ka Nchekwa Sie Ike

```bash
# Opekempe nchekwa maka mmepụta:
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # Na-achọ HTTPS
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Kwenye njirimara oku proxy niile
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Ekpughela key na UI ma ọlị
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # Oke bụ 5 MB
```

---

## 5. Nhichapụ Ihe Ntinye & Nchedo PII

OmniRoute na-enye nchedo nwere ọkwa abụọ: nyocha ntụnye ọjọọ n'akụkụ arịrịọ na iwepụ PII n'akụkụ nzaghachi.

> **⚠️ Oke ya:** Usoro nchedo ndị a bụ nchọpụta _heuristic nke a na-agba mbọ ime nke ọma_, ọ bụghị firewall zuru oke megide ntụnye ọjọọ na prompt ma ọ bụ sistemụ PII DLP. Ha nwere ike iwepụta nchọpụta ụgha (dịka ịkọpụta prompt persona/RPG na-enweghị nsogbu dịka nke ọjọọ) na enweghị nchọpụta mgbe ihe ọjọọ dị (dịka leetspeak, oghere dị iche iche, na usoro na-abụghị Bekee). Naanị ha ezughị maka irube isi n'ụkpụrụ. Hazie mode ndị ahụ ma nwalee ha megide traffic gị tupu ịdabere na ha.

### N'akụkụ Arịrịọ: Nchedo Megide Prompt Injection

| Variable                          | Nke Ndabara  | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                |
| --------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Na-eme ka nyocha ozi ndị na-abata maka usoro prompt injection rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                               |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Iwu injection: `warn` = dekọọ naanị, `block` = jụ arịrịọ site na 400. `redact` ochie **anaghị** ewepụ ederede injection; jiri `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` maka idegharị PII dị n'arịrịọ. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(ahazighị)_ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` — otu omume ahụ.                                                                                                                        |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`       | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Ogo ihe ize ndụ kacha nta nke `MODE=block` ga-ajụ: `high` (ndabara), `medium`, ma ọ bụ `low`. A na-elele usoro `medium` naanị ma ọ bụrụ na e belataghị ya.                           |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(ahazighị)_ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` — otu omume ahụ.                                                                                                             |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, zoo PII n'arịrịọ ndị na-abata (ọ naghị adabere na mode injection).                                                                                                 |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Zoo usoro API-key / secret-token ndị a ma ama na payload arịrịọ/nzaghachi. A ga-ahọrọ ya ka ọ rụọ ọrụ; ọ na-eme dịka `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                                        |

### N'akụkụ Nzaghachi: Ihe Nhichapụ PII

| Variable                         | Nke Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite         | Nkọwa                                                                        |
| -------------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`     | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Nyochaa nzaghachi LLM maka PII gbapụrụ tupu iziga ya nye client.             |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact`    | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = zoo PII, `warn` = dekọọ naanị, `block` = tụfuo nzaghachi ahụ dum. |

### Ihe Nhichapụ Context nke Tokenized-Route na VS Code

| Variable                            | Nke Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`         | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Na-ewepụ context implicit nke active-editor (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) n'arịrịọ `/v1/vscode/[token]/*` ma zoo ọdịnaya faịlụ nwere mmetụta dị elu ndị e tinyere n'ụzọ doro anya. Ọ dị nchebe na ndabara; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya. |

### Ọnọdụ Ojiji

| Ọnọdụ Ojiji                    | Nhazi                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Irube isi n'ụkpụrụ ụlọ ọrụ** | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (igbochi injection + izochi PII dị n'arịrịọ/nzaghachi; mode ndị ahụ anaghị adabere ibe ha) |
| **Nlekota naanị**              | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — na-edekọ mana ọ naghị egbochi mgbe ọ bụla                                                                                                                 |
| **Ojiji nkeonwe**              | Hapụ ha niile ka ha ghara ịrụ ọrụ — enweghị overhead ọ bụla                                                                                                                                                             |

---

## 6. Iwu Ngwaọrụ & Nduzi Ụzọ

| Mgbanwe                                                     | Ndabara                      | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                              | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                   | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                        | Na-achịkwa ohere LLM nwere iji ngwaọrụ/ịkpọ ọrụ. `allowlist` = naanị ngwaọrụ ndị edepụtara, `denylist` = ha niile ma e wezụga ndị edepụtara, `disabled` = enweghị mmachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json` | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`            | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ JSON nke iwu mgbanwe payload (mmegharị upstream pụrụ iche maka model/protocol ọ bụla).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                       | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`            | Oge etiti (ms) maka ibugharị faịlụ iwu payload ọzọ ozugbo mgbanwe mere. Nke kacha nta bụ `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                   | Nhọrọ a na-ahọrọ itinye n'ọrụ: duzie ID model `claude-*` na-enweghị prefix sitere na ndị ahịa Claude Code site na akaụntụ OAuth Claude Code kama ịchọ prefix provider. Prefix provider e depụtara kpọmkwem ka na-ebute ụzọ. A pụkwara ịhazi ya site na mgba ọkụ dashboard dị na ibe provider Claude.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `BARE_MODEL_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_LEGACY`                        | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                   | Omume ochie `resolveBareModelToConnectionDefault` (#4825): jiri `defaultModel` nke njikọ ahọpụtara dochie aha model ọ bụla na-enweghị prefix. A na-agbanyụ ya na ndabara; naanị `auto` ma ọ bụ model efu ka a na-edochi, ọ na-edekwa otu ịdọ aka ná ntị maka ụzọ abụọ ọ bụla mgbe agbanyere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                          | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`             | Oke arịrịọ na-aga n'otu oge maka model ọ bụla n'ime ngwakọta round-robin (#9100). E tinyere oke siri ike nke arịrịọ 3 na-aga n'otu oge maka model ọ bụla na semaphore ngwakọta round-robin, na-enweghị ụzọ ịgbanwe ya, nke mere ka okporo ụzọ nwere concurrency dị elu chere n'azụ oke ahụ n'usoro. A na-enyocha ka ọ bụrụ `>= 1`, ma na-egbochi ya ka ọ ghara ịgafe `<= 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                      | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`                | Nhọrọ dị ize ndụ nke na-amafere nyocha context-window / max-input-token nke OmniRoute n'ime mpaghara maka arịrịọ kpọmkwem nke otu model. Ndị provider upstream ka na-amanye oke ha n'ezie; mkpakọ prompt na oke output-token nke model n'onwe ya ka na-arụ ọrụ. Usoro mbute dị irè bụ ngbanwe Feature Flags DB > mgbanwe gburugburu ebe obibi > ndabara; achọghị ịmalitegharịa.                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`                           | _(edobeghị)_                 | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`         | Dọkụmentị YAML `providers:` dị n'ime ya (RIC-738, D4). Mgbe edobere ya (ma ọ nwere ma ọ bụ enweghị ngọngọ `strategy:`), `/v1/chat/completions` na-atụgharị gaa na ebe ntinye self-hosted jikọtara ọnụ nke kwekọrọ na OpenAI kama pipeline igwe ojii. Mgbe edobeghị ya (ndabara): ụzọ ahụ na-aga ozugbo na pipeline igwe ojii dị ugbu a. Lee `docs/routing/SELF_HOSTED_OPENAI_ENTRY.md`.                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS_FILE`                      | _(edobeghị)_                 | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`         | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ YAML nke nwere otu dọkụmentị `providers:` ahụ dị na `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, maka ntinye sistemụ ndị na-ahọrọ faịlụ karịa env var dị n'ime ya. Nke ọ bụla n'ime ha na-eme ka ebe ntinye self-hosted rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_API_KEY`                             | _(edobeghị — ụzọ ghe oghe)_  | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`         | Igodo API nkekọrịta a na-ahọrọ maka ebe ntinye self-hosted jikọtara ọnụ (ntọala D5, e debere maka sistemụ oke ojiji nke igodo ọ bụla). Mgbe edobere ya, arịrịọ ga-etinyerịrị `Authorization: Bearer <key>`. Mgbe edobeghị ya: ụzọ ahụ ghe oghe, dịka ụkpụrụ local-provider self-hosted dị ugbu a (ntinye sistemụ loopback/netwọkụ ntụkwasị obi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`                            | _(edobeghị)_                 | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`       | Dọkụmentị YAML `strategy:` dị n'ime ya maka injin nduzi ụzọ nwere nsonaazụ a kapịrị ọnụ (M2/RIC-740, D3) — blacklist/whitelist, cooldown breaker, ibute ọnụ ahịa ụzọ, ịtụle latency, na usoro fallback. Ọ na-akagbu ngọngọ `strategy:` dị n'ime `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, maka igodo ọ bụla. Lee `docs/routing/DETERMINISTIC_ROUTING.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY_FILE`                       | _(edoghị ya)_                | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`       | Ụzọ faịlụ YAML nke nwere otu dọkụmentị `strategy:` ahụ dị na `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`, maka nrụnye ndị họọrọ faịlụ karịa env var e dere ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(edoghị ya)_                | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts`     | Tọọ `1` iji kwụsị ịchịkọta akụkọ mkparịta ụka. `resolveConversationId()` na-eweghachi nsonaazụ a na-esoghị tupu ọ gụọ SQLite ma ọ bụ nyochaa akụkọ ozi, nke a metụtakwara NJ nnọkọ ndị klayent nyere. Nhazi nnọkọ ntụgharị agbanweghị, a naghịkwa ehichapụ ndekọ ndị dịbu. Maka nrụnye ndị na-adịghị eji nlele mkparịta ụka dị na dashboard ma chọọ ka tebụl ntụgharị kwụsị ịba ụba.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_API_KEY`                                   | _(edoghị ya)_                | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts` | Igodo API maka atụmatụ router auto-combo `nadir` a na-ahọrọ isonye na ya, nke a na-eji mgbe edoghị `config.nadir.apiKey` nke combo ahụ n'onwe ya. Nhọrọ: oku ndị na-enweghị igodo na-abanye n'ọkwa anonimọs nke Nadir, nke nwere mmachi ọnụego n'otu IP, ya mere enweghị ike iji ya n'azụ gateway a na-ekekọrịta. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; họrọ oghere nke combo ọ bụla mgbe combo dị iche iche na-eji akaụntụ dị iche iche.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_BASE_URL`                                  | `https://api.getnadir.com`   | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts` | Isi origin nke API mkpebi Nadir, nke a na-eji mgbe edoghị `config.nadir.baseUrl` nke combo ahụ n'onwe ya. A chọrọ ya naanị maka Nadir a na-akwado n'onwe gị, nke na-edobe isi prompt n'ime netwọkụ nke gị. A na-anabata `/v1` dị n'azụ ma wepụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW`                           | `provider`                   | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`           | Oge windo kwa izu maka oke ojiji USD nke igodo API ọ bụla (mmanye arịrịọ, `GET /api/keys/{id}/usage-limits`, `@@om-usage`). `provider`: izu ahụ na-agwụ n'oge nrụgharị kwa izu kacha nso n'etiti njikọ upstream igodo ahụ nwere ike iru, ma ọ bụ bụrụ ụbọchị 7 na-agagharị mgbe a maghị nke ọ bụla. `calendar`: izu mpaghara site na Mọnde 00:00 ruo Mọnde na-esote 00:00, nke bụ otu maka igodo niile. A na-atụ aro `calendar` mgbe ọtụtụ igodo nwere mmefu ego nke ha na-ekekọrịta otu nchịkọta akaụntụ upstream. Uru ndị na-adịghị irè na-alaghachi na `provider`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW_TIMEZONE`                  | _(mpaghara oge nke usoro)_   | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`           | Mpaghara oge IANA (dịka ọmụmaatụ `Asia/Shanghai`) nke na-akọwa Mọnde 00:00 maka windo kwa izu `calendar`. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya ma ọ bụ na ọ dịghị irè: mpaghara oge nke usoro OmniRoute (`TZ` nke host ma ọ bụ container, nke a na-ahapụkarị n'enweghị ntọala, ya bụ, Oge Ụwa A Haziri Ahazi, n'ime image container). A na-eleghara ya anya na ọnọdụ `provider`.                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_TOOLS_LIMIT`                                 | `128`                        | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                 | Ọnụọgụ ngwaọrụ kachasị elu a na-ekwe na arịrịọ na ndabara (ndabara OpenAI). Naanị integer ndị karịrị efu; uru edoghị ma ọ bụ na-adịghị irè ga-edobe `128`. Provider nwere oke amaara/proactive (`grok-cli` 200, `nvidia` 1536) ka ga-etinye oke nke ya — nke a na-agbanwe naanị elu ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROMPT_EMULATE_TOOLS`                            | _(agbanye)_                  | `open-sse/executors/syntxChat.ts`              | Ịkpọ ngwaọrụ e ji prompt ṅomie nke SYNTX.ai: ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya ma ọ bụ bụrụ `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes`, a ga-etinye katalọgụ ngwaọrụ mpaghara n'ime prompt; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (dịka `0`) ga-amafe ntinye ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
