| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`    | `7`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Onye na-eweta API-key na-abanye na DEGRADED mgbe ọdịda ruru ọnụ ọgụgụ a.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`   | `4`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kachasị elu eji amụba ịrị elu resetTimeout nke onye na-eweta API-key.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT` | `3`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Onye na-eweta API-key na-abawanye nkwụsị mgbe okirikiri mmeghe ruru ọnụ ọgụgụ a.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Breaker n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọdịda tupu onye na-eweta nke ime obodo niile abanye n'oge izu ike.                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Breaker n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: windo ngụkọ ọdịda na-agagharị (ms) maka ndị na-eweta nke ime obodo.                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Breaker n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: oge izu ike (ms) ozugbo e ruru oke onye na-eweta nke ime obodo.                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`                               | `60000`   | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts` | Oge izu ike nke igodo ọ bụla (ms), nke ntụgharị API-key nwere ọtụtụ igodo na-etinye mgbe 429 sitere n'aka upstream enweghị `Retry-After` (#14573). Ụkpụrụ na-abụghị nke dị mma ma ọ bụ na-abụghị ọnụọgụ ga-alaghachi na ndabara. |
| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`         | Omimi backoff nke a na-ewere na onye na-eweta nke pin context-cache adịghị mma ruo ogologo oge, wee tụfuo pin ahụ maka failover.                                                                                                 |
| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`         | Windo mgbochi ịgbanwegharị ugboro ugboro (ms) nke na-anabata obere oge izu ike na-adịru nwa oge tupu a tụfuo pin context-cache.                                                                                                  |

### Ọnọdụ ojiji

| Ọnọdụ ojiji                            | Nhazi                                                    |
| -------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Mmepụta koodu na-ewe ogologo oge**   | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (nkeji 15)                   |
| **Kpaara ngụkọta oge ndụ stream ókè**  | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (nkeji 21)            |
| **Ịda ngwa ngwa maka API mmepụta**     | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                      |
| **Ụdịdị nwere echiche gbatịrị agbatị** | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (nkeji 5 n'etiti chunks) |

---

## 16. Ndekọ

Sistemụ ndekọ na-ede ma na stdout ma na faịlụ ndekọ ndị a na-atụgharịgharị. A na-agụ nhazi niile site na `src/lib/logEnv.ts`.

| Mgbanwe                                   | Ndabara                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Ọkwa ndekọ kacha nta: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Ọdịdị mmepụta: `text` (nke mmadụ nwere ike ịgụ) ma ọ bụ `json` (ahaziri ahazi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Dee ndekọ na faịlụ yana stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Ụzọ faịlụ ndekọ (n'ihe metụtara mgbọrọgwụ prọjektị ma ọ bụ `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Ogo faịlụ kachasị tupu ntụgharị. Ọ na-anabata: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, ma ọ bụ bytes nkịtị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Ụbọchị ole a ga-edowe faịlụ ndekọ ngwa ndị a tụgharịworo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Ọnụọgụ ndabere faịlụ ndekọ ndị a tụgharịworo kachasị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Ụbọchị ole a ga-edowe ndenye ndekọ arịrịọ/oku na ebe nchekwa data.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Ọnụọgụ ndenye ndekọ oku kachasị na buffer dị na ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Ahịrị kachasị na tebụl SQLite `call_logs` tupu ebelata ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(edoghị ya)_              | Manye ndekọ arịrịọ zuru ezu ka ọ gbanye ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ, na-akagbu ntọala dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (1 elekere)      | Ogologo oge kachasị maka ndenye ndekọ arịrịọ nọ n'ọrụ ma bụrụ nke a hapụrụ tupu nhicha n'ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Chekwaa iberibe stream na pipeline artifacts mgbe `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Nhọrọ nke onye ọrụ ga-eme (`true`) — agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara iji chekwaa ohere diski.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Ogo artifact ndekọ oku pipeline kachasị na KB mgbe `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Ahịrị kachasị na tebụl SQLite `proxy_logs` tupu ebelata ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Tinye IP ndị ahịa/egress na prefix akaụntụ na ndekọ console `[ProxyEgress]`. Ihe ndekọ proxy-log nke dashboard/ebe nchekwa data na-edowe nkọwa niile.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_LOG_FIRST_CHUNK_TIMING`            | `false`                    | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` ma ọ bụ `"1"` iji dekọọ `first_chunk_ms` (site na mbido izipu ruo na byte mbụ bara uru nke body) n'ahịrị proxy-log. Ọ na-etinye body upstream ọ bụla n'ime stream passthrough umengwụ ma na-eme patch n'ahịrị ahụ ozugbo byte ahụ rutere; agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara ka response gafee n'enweghị mgbanwe. |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (1 nkeji)          | Ugboro ole `src/lib/logRotation.ts` na-enyochagharị nha faịlụ ndekọ nọ n'ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Ogologo string kachasị a na-edowe na chat log artifacts (ndabara bụ 64 KB).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Ọnụọgụ ihe array a na-edowe site na ngwụcha mgbe a na-ebipụ chat log payloads.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Omimi ntinye kachasị tupu ebipụ chat log payloads.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Object keys kachasị a na-edowe na chat log payloads (0 = enweghị oke).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Nha body arịrịọ/response dum (KB) tupu e jiri nchịkọta nkịtị dochie ya kama clone zuru ezu. Welie nke a ma ọ bụrụ na mkparịta ụka agentic ogologo na-egosi placeholder kama ozi ndị ahụ n'ezie na dashboard.                                                                                                                     |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Mgbe ọ bụ true, `serializeArtifactForStorage` na-amafere mbipụ dabere na nha. Maka debug naanị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

## 17. Nkwalite Ebe Nchekwa

| Agbanwe                       | Ndabara                                                                                      | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`         | _akpaka_ (igwe nkịtị); **`1024` n’ime oyiyi Docker**                                         | Oke heap V8 (MB) **a tụrụ aro** maka Docker/standalone. Mgbe edoghị ya, a na-ahazi ya n’akpaka (~35% nke RAM sistemụ, ma debe ya n’etiti `[512, 4096]`); `512` bụ naanị oke ala mgbe enweghị ike ịgụ ebe nchekwa niile. Na `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), a na-agbakwunye uru e nyere **n'ụzọ doro anya** dịka `--max-old-space-size`, ọ ga-enwekwa **mmeri** karịa ọkọlọtọ heap NODE_OPTIONS na-emegide ya (ọkọlọtọ ikpeazụ V8). `omniroute serve` ka na-ahọrọ ọkọlọtọ heap NODE_OPTIONS dị adị (#5238). Edokwala ha abụọ ka ha nwee ọnụọgụ dị iche — usoro ahụ na-edekọ ịdọ aka ná ntị nke na-akpọ uru abụọ ahụ na nke meriri. **Oyiyi Docker gọọmentị na-edobe `1024` mgbe niile, ya mere nhazi akpaka anaghị arụ ọrụ n’ebe ahụ.** Coding-agent `/v1/responses` chọrọ `8192`–`12288` tinyere ohere mgbakwunye cgroup — lee [Ntuziaka Docker — RAM oge ọrụ](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `OMNIROUTE_RSS_PRESSURE_MB`   | _akpaka_ (`max(3072, 2 × OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB)`; agbanyụrụ mgbe edoghị `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`) | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Fuse zuru oke nke RSS usoro (MB) maka ebe nchekwa native/external nke V8 `heapUsed` enweghị ike ịhụ; mgbe ọ gafere ya, arịrịọ ọhụrụ na-enweta 503 `resource_pressure`. RSS anaghị adịkarị agbada mgbe ọ rịgochara na mberede, ya mere jiri ya na `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`. |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`       | `50`                                                                                         | Ọnụọgụ kachasị elu nke ndenye system prompt echekwara na cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`      | `2097152` (2 MB)                                                                             | Oke nha mkpokọta cache prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`         | `300000` (nkeji 5)                                                                           | TTL nke ndenye cache prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`     | `100`                                                                                        | Ọnụọgụ kachasị elu nke nzaghachi temperature=0 echekwara na cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`    | `4194304` (4 MB)                                                                             | Oke nha mkpokọta cache semantic.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`       | `1800000` (nkeji 30)                                                                         | TTL nke ndenye cache semantic.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE` | `5`                                                                                          | Mgbọrọgwụ local-corpus ndị na-edobe index dị ndụ n’ebe nchekwa n’otu oge (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: mgbe eruola oke ahụ, a na-ewepụ index nke mgbọrọgwụ ejiribeghị kemgbe ogologo oge ma wughachi ya n’ajụjụ ya na-esote. A na-amachi ya ka ọ ghara ịdị n’okpuru `1`; uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-alaghachi na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`          | `50`                                                                                         | Ọnụọgụ kachasị elu nke mmemme stream ọhụrụ n’ime buffer nlele ozugbo nke Dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`      | _(edoghị)_                                                                                   | Mgbanwe zuru ụwa ọnụ nke ogologo context (tokens) maka provider ọ bụla na-enweghị agbanwe `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`. Mgbe edobere ya, ọ **na-anọchi** catalog, mgbanwe Dashboard na uru registry maka provider ndị ahụ; mgbe edoghị ya, OmniRoute na-eji usoro arụnyere n’ime ya, nke nhọrọ ikpeazụ ya bụ `128000`. Lee [Ogologo context maka provider ọ bụla](#per-provider-context-length-context_length_provider).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`          | `100`                                                                                        | Ohere token mgbakwunye edobere iche mgbe a na-enyocha oke ojiji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

### Ogologo context maka provider ọ bụla (`CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`)

`open-sse/services/contextManager.ts` (`getEnvOverride()` / `resolveTokenLimit()`) na-agụkwa ụdị
**maka provider ọ bụla** nke a na-ewepụta aha ya site na ID provider n’oge ọrụ, ya mere enweghị ike
ịdepụta ya ahịrị n’ahịrị na tebụl dị n’elu ma ọ bụ na `.env.example`.

**Aha.** `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`, ebe `<PROVIDER>` bụ ID onye na-eweta ahụ e dere n’ mkpụrụedemede ukwu, ma jiri `_` dochie
mkpụrụedemede ọ bụla na-abụghị `A-Z` / `0-9`. ID onye na-eweta ahụ bụ nke iwu kwadoro mgbe
e mechara idozi utuaha (akụkụ `provider` nke eriri `provider/model` mgbe e dozichara utuaha dị mkpirikpi).
Njikọ omenala dakọtara na OpenAI ma ọ bụ Anthropic na-eji ID njikọ e mepụtara maka ha, ya mere
UUID bụ akụkụ nke aha mgbanwe ahụ. Uru ahụ bụ ọnụọgụ token dị mma; a na-eleghara ihe ọ bụla
ọzọ anya (ihe efu, `0`, ọnụọgụ na-adịghị mma, ihe na-abụghị ọnụọgụ), idozi ahụ wee gaa n’usoro na-esote.

| ID onye na-eweta                                            | Aha mgbanwe                                                                |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| agentrouter                                                 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER                                                 |
| openrouter                                                  | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENROUTER                                                  |
| openai-compatible-chat-0f8e2c1a-5b7d-4e3f-9a6c-2d1b8e4f7a90 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE_CHAT_0F8E2C1A_5B7D_4E3F_9A6C_2D1B8E4F7A90 |

**Usoro mkpa.** Maka ụzọ abụọ `provider/model`, nzọụkwụ mbụ nke wepụtara uru bụ nke a ga-eji:

1. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>` maka onye na-eweta ahụ.
2. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT` (mgbanwe gburugburu zuru ụwa ọnụ nke na-emeri ndị ọzọ).
3. Windo nke otu model: ngbanwe context echekwara (nke edobere na dashboard, ma ọ bụ nke
   onye nhazi nchọpụta kegidere na-akpaghị aka, lee `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL`), ma ọ bụghị ya, uru
   catalog / models.dev emekọrịtara.
4. `defaultContextLength` nke ndekọ onye na-eweta ahụ.
5. Usoro ntule dabere n’aha model (`claude` 200000, `gemini` 1000000, `gpt` / `o1` / `o3` / `o4` /
   `codex` 400000).
6. Ndabara arụnyere n’ime usoro maka onye na-eweta ọ bụla, emesịa `128000`.

Nzọụkwụ gburugburu abụọ ahụ metụtara **onye na-eweta dum**: model ọ bụla nke onye na-eweta ahụ na-enweta
otu oke ahụ, ha na-emerikwa ngbanwe dashboard nke otu model. Mgbe naanị otu model nke onye na-eweta
chọrọ windo dị iche, jiri ngbanwe nke otu model na dashboard kama.

**Oke ọrụ.** Usoro a na-achị nyocha windo context n’oge arịrịọ (ọjụjụ
`context_length_exceeded` / "Ntinye gafere windo context" dị na
`open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`), oke mmefu mkpakọ prompt na oke context ngwakọta. `context_length`
nke `GET /v1/models` na-egosi ka nwere ike isi na metadata catalog ma ọ bụ registry
pụta, ọ bụghịkwa ihe e kwere nkwa na ọ ga-egosipụta ngbanwe gburugburu ahụ.

**Ọmụmaatụ.** Onye na-eweta nke model ya na-anabata windo token 1,050,000 ebe catalog na-egosi
128,000:

```dotenv
CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER=1050000
```

Malitegharịa server mgbe ị gbanwechara ya: a na-agụ uru ahụ site na gburugburu process nke server.
Na **ngwa desktop**, server na-agba dịka child process, ọ naghị ahụkwa system variable
edobere mgbe e bidoro ngwa ahụ; tinye ahịrị ahụ na faịlụ `.env` nke ngwa ahụ na-ebunye (usoro nchọta dị na
[Electron Guide: Nchọta faịlụ gburugburu](../guides/ELECTRON_GUIDE.md#environment-file-lookup)),
wee mechie ngwa ahụ kpamkpam ma maliteghachi ya.

### Mkpakọ

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | edoghị ya | Tụkwasị `.rtk/filters.json` nke project obi na-enweghị hash `.rtk/trust.json`. Jiri naanị na mmepe mpaghara a na-achịkwa. |

### Injin Ncheta (atụmatụ 21)

### Ọnụ ahịa event-loop nke ncheta, nkà, na mmegharị token (#10349)

OmniRoute bụ **otu Node process**. Mwepụta/nweta ncheta, ntinye nkà, na mmegharị token onye na-eweta na-agba na **otu event loop ahụ** dị ka `GET /healthz` na dashboard. Ha abụghị worker thread.

| Ọrụ                     | Koodu                              | Ndabara                                                   | Njikwa onye na-arụ sistemụ                                                                                                          |
| ----------------------- | ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Mwepụta / nnweta ncheta | `src/lib/memory/`                  | **memoryEnabled** dị na dashboard (ndabara bụ ịgbanwu ya) | Gbanyụọ **Settings → Memory**. Enweghị env kill switch pụrụ iche ma e wezụga ịgbanyụ atụmatụ ahụ na settings.                       |
| Ntinye nkà              | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | **skillsEnabled** dị na dashboard (ndabara bụ ịgbanwu ya) | Gbanyụọ **Settings → Memory/Skills** (`skillsEnabled`). Ntọala sandbox dị n’okpuru na-amachi naanị mmezu mgbe agbanwuru ntinye ahụ. |
| Mmegharị token          | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | Agbanwuru ya maka ndị na-eweta OAuth/web ejikọrọ          | Kwụsị njikọ onye na-eweta ahụ ma ọ bụ hapụ token ka ha nọgide na-arụ ọrụ; enweghị env `TOKEN_REFRESH=0` ugbu a.                     |

Ọ bụrụ na `/healthz` na-egbu oge n’igwe na-adịghị arụ nnukwu ọrụ, buru ụzọ gbanyụọ ncheta + nkà, wee lelee ibu catalog/mkpakọ (#10303, #9685). Atụmatụ ndị a na-ahapụ ohere n’ebe `await` dị, mana ha ka na-asọrịta mpi maka otu thread ahụ.

Ntọala embedding layer, vector store na reranking maka obere sistemụ ncheta na-adịgide adịgide (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Mgbanwe                              | Ndabara                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (nkeji 5)          | TTL maka cache embedding dị na ebe nchekwa (n’otu mbido/model/akara dim).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | Ọnụ ọgụgụ ndenye LRU kachasị elu a na-edobe na cache embedding.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | ID repo HF maka pipeline MiniLM mpaghara `@huggingface/transformers` nke a ga-ahọrọ iji (~23 MB int8, ~400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | ID repo HF maka embedder tebụl nchọ static potion/Model2Vec. A na-ebudata ya nwayọ mgbe achọrọ ya n'ime ndekọ cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | Ndekọ a na-eji echekwa faịlụ model static potion na cache. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, ndabara ya dị n'okpuru `DATA_DIR`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Dochie URL ntọala Hugging Face Hub nke `staticPotion.ts` na-eji (dịka endpoint mirror maka nhazi air-gapped).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | Top-K ndabara nke ọchụchọ vector brute-force `sqlite-vec` na-eji n'ime `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Constant `k` nke Reciprocal Rank Fusion maka iweghachite ngwakọta FTS5 + vector (usoro sqlite-vec).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Ebe njikọ ule/nchọpụta nsogbu n'ime `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): mgbe ọ bụ `true`, ọ na-amanye vector store ka ọ bụrụ `null` (na-eme ka gburugburu cloud/WASM na-enweghị `sqlite-vec`), na-ewedata iweghachite ebe nchekwa ka ọ bụrụ ọchụchọ keyword FTS5. Hapụ ya na-enweghị ntọala na production.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(edoghị ya)_               | API key maka backend Notion (`genericBackend.ts` known backend preset na-eji ya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | URL ntọala maka API Notion (enwere ike dochie ya maka nhọrọ Notion a na-akwado n'onwe ya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(edoghị ya)_               | API key maka backend Obsidian Vault (`genericBackend.ts` known backend preset na-eji ya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | URL ntọala maka API Obsidian Vault (enwere ike dochie ya maka vault dị anya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | Isi switch nke mbelata ebe nchekwa typed TV6. **A ga-ahọrọ iji ya (ndabara bụ gbanyụọ)** — sweep ahụ **na-ehichapụ** ebe nchekwa ndị belatara. Mgbe ọ gbanyụrụ, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` bụ naanị telemetry, ọ dịghịkwa ihe a na-ehichapụ ma ọlị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (ụbọchị) nke ebe nchekwa `episodic` a na-ejighị eme ihe ga-emesịa belata. `0` na-emekwa ka episodic ghara imetụta ya. Ụdị na-adịgide adịgide (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) anaghị emetụta ya mgbe niile. Elekere mbelata ahụ na-amalitegharị dabere na `last_accessed_at`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Ebe nchekwa etinyere ugboro `>=` ọnụ ọgụgụ a agaghị emetụta mbelata n'agbanyeghị ụdị ya. `0` na-gbanyụọ nchedo sitere na nnweta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (agbanyụrụ)             | Oge etiti (sekọnd) maka sweep mbelata oge niile a na-ahọrọ iji n'ime `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/edoghị ya = enweghị sweep oge niile. A ga-ahọrọ ya ugboro abụọ: ọ chọkwara `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(edoghị ya)_               | ID provider ndị e ji rikoma kewaa (anaghị ele mkpụrụedemede ukwu/nta anya) nke na-anabata ozi `system` **naanị na index 0** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Maka ndị a, splice ebe nchekwa dị n'etiti array nke na-echekwa cache adịghị mma na mkparịta ụka nwere ọtụtụ turn, ya mere a na-ejikọta/etinye ebe nchekwa n'ihu dịka ozi system mbụ. Ndabara bụ naanị `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo`; gbasaa ya maka endpoint OpenAI-compatible a na-akwado n'onwe ya (dịka Qwen3.5+/3.6) nke chat template ya na-amanye otu mmachi ozi system mbụ ahụ. |

### Ihe Nlereanya Docker nwere RAM Dị Ala

`128` bụ naanị maka dashboard. Coding agents na heap a na-enweta `FATAL ERROR` n'oge `/v1/responses` dị ogologo. Ejila ihe nlereanya a dịka gateway Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

| Mgbanwe                 | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta        | Nkọwa                                            |
| ----------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `PRICING_SYNC_ENABLED`  | `false`       | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Mmekọrịta ọnụahịa oge ọbụla a na-ahọrọ ịgbalite. |
| `PRICING_SYNC_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Oge dị n'etiti mmekọrịta na sekọnd.              |
| `PRICING_SYNC_SOURCES`  | `litellm`     | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Isi mmalite data ndị rikoma kewara.              |

## Mmekọrịta Arena ELO

| Mgbanwe                      | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                                                                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ---------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_ENABLED`     | `true`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                     | Mmekọrịta ELO nke bọọdụ ndị-isi Arena AI n'oge ụfọdụ, nke a pụrụ ịhazi site na Dashboard Feature Flags ma ọ bụ jiri `false` kwụsị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `MODELS_CATALOG_PREFIX_MODE` | `dual`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/models/catalog.ts` | Ụdị prefix a na-eji maka id ụdịdị na `GET /v1/models`. `dual` na-akpọsa ma prefix alias dị mkpụmkpụ ma prefix provider canonical maka ụdịdị ọ bụla (ndakọrịta azụ — ọ fọrọ nke nta ka ọ bụrụ na ọ na-eme katalọgụ ahụ okpukpu abụọ); `alias` na-ewepụta otu id maka ụdịdị ọ bụla; `canonical` na-ewepụta naanị prefix provider-id zuru ezu (providers nke alias ha bụrịrị id canonical na-edobe otu ndenye ha). Clients nwere ike iji `?prefix=alias` gbanwee ya maka arịrịọ ọ bụla. Lee [API_REFERENCE](API_REFERENCE.md#model-id-prefixes-prefix). |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_INTERVAL`    | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/arenaEloSync.ts`                                                            | Oge dị n'etiti mmekọrịta na sekọnd.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

## Provider PromptQL Playground (Na-abụghị nke Gọọmentị/Nnwale)

Njikọ nnọkọ GraphQL e ji reverse engineering mepụta maka prompt.ql.app (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Ha niile bụ nhọrọ — ndabara na-arụtụ aka na endpoints playground ọha; gbanwee naanị maka deployment PromptQL nke ị na-akwado n'onwe gị/ọzọ.

| Mgbanwe                      | Ndabara                                                            | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                                                       | Nkọwa                                                          |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROMPTQL_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.prompt.ql.app/promptql/playground-v2-hge/v1/graphql` | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Endpoint GraphQL e ji arụ ọrụ nkata/nnọkọ.                     |
| `PROMPTQL_CREDITS_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.pro.ql.app/v1/graphql`                               | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`, `open-sse/services/usage/promptql.ts` | Endpoint GraphQL e ji ajụ banyere itule kredit/ojiji.          |
| `PROMPTQL_TOKEN_REFRESH_URL` | `https://auth.pro.ql.app/ddn/project/token`                        | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Endpoint e ji anwa ime ka token dị ọhụrụ.                      |
| `PROMPTQL_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `180000`                                                           | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Oge kachasị (ms) iji na-enyocha `thread_events` tupu oge agwụ. |

## Onye Na-eweta Weebụ Adobe Firefly (Nke Na-abụghị nke Gọọmenti/Nnwale)

Mmegharị nnọkọ nke ihe nchọgharị na-achịkwa maka onye na-eweta weebụ Adobe Firefly
(`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`,
`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`). Ọ bụ nhọrọ — a haziri ndabara niile maka nrụnye
desktọpụ nkịtị.

> **E wepụrụ ya na #9255.** E ji ọrụ nbanye ihe nchọgharị Playwright dochie sistemụ Chrome ochie e jikọrọ na CDP (adobeFireflyChromeRuntime.ts), ya mere ntọala njikwa ya adịghịzi. Ngbanwe
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_ CDP_PORT / VISIBLE / HEADED / PING / FORCE_RESTART, tinyere
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_LOGIN_WAIT_MS na ADOBE_FIREFLY_FORTER_WAIT_MS, anaghị agụ ebe ọ bụla n'ime
> codebase — ịtọ ha enweghị mmetụta ọ bụla.

| Ngbanwe                              | Ndabara    | Faịlụ Isi                                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------ | ---------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`      | `0`        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` maka Chrome headless n'ezie (a maara na ọ naghị arụ ọrụ maka generate; maka debugging naanị).                         |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`      | `1`        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Ịhọrọ ịgbanye/gbanyụọ ikpo ihe nchọgharị ọkụ tupu oge eruo. `0` na-akwụsị ikpo ọkụ tupu oge eruo (mgbake 408 n'etiti batch ka na-arụ ọrụ). |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`         | `1`        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji kwụsị ichekwa nnọkọ Adobe Firefly na diski.                                                                       |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`    | _(edoghị)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Oghere kacha nta (ms) a na-amanye n'etiti submit ndị na-esochi ibe ha, nke na-anọchi ndabara arụnyere.                                     |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`   | _(edoghị)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Oghere mgbakwunye (ms) a na-etinye mgbe batch gara nke ọma, nke na-anọchi ndabara arụnyere.                                                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS` | _(edoghị)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`       | Oge nchere ntọala (ms) tupu izipu arịrịọ generation, nke na-anọchi ndabara arụnyere.                                                       |

## 19. Mmekọrịta Model (Dev)

| Mgbanwe                             | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(edobeghị)_  | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Nchịkwa siri ike maka mmekọrịta ọnụahịa models.dev. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya = soro Ntọala > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no` **na-emeri DB** ma na-amafere ma mmekọrịta oge niile ma nyocha SQL/JSON nke `getModelsDevPricing()` (mgbake mgbe dashboard rapaara n'otu event loop ahụ). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` na-amanye ka mmekọrịta rụọ ọrụ. Nchekwa/nhichapụ ọnụahịa ka na-akpọ `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, nke anaghị eme ihe n'okpuru mmachi nke nkeji 60 ma ọ bụ `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Ogologo oge n'etiti mmekọrịta katalọgụ model n'oge mmepe, na sekọnd.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Ogologo oge (na sekọnd) maka ihe mmezi context-window nke na-emezi onwe ya (5004): ọ na-edobe windo ndị provider kwupụtara site na nchọpụta `/models` dịka mgbanwe `auto:discovery` mgbe ha dị iche na katalọgụ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya. Ọ na-eji data emekọrịtalarị ọzọ (enweghị fetch ọhụrụ); ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-edegharị mgbanwe `manual`.                                                                                                                                                            |

| Mgbanwe                                        | Ndabara                                | Faịlụ Isi                                                                          | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OPENROUTER_CATALOG_TTL_MS`                    | `86400000` (awa 24)                    | `src/lib/catalog/openrouterCatalog.ts`                                             | TTL nke cache katalọgụ ụdị OpenRouter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MODEL_CATALOG_INCLUDE_NAMES`                  | `true`                                 | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                   | Tinye oghere `name` ndị dị mfe igosi na nzaghachi `/v1/models`. Gbanyụọ ya maka ndị ahịa na-atụ anya naanị ID.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CATALOG_BUILD_TIMEOUT_MS`                     | `8000` (sekọnd 8)                      | `src/app/api/v1/models/catalogCache.ts`                                            | Oke oge nchere cold-path maka iwughachi katalọgụ `GET /v1/models` agbakọtara ọnụ (#12627). Mgbe oge gafere, a na-enye nzaghachi 200 ikpeazụ dị mma ma ọ bụrụ na ọ dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNCED_CATALOG_STALE_AFTER_MS`      | `2592000000` (ụbọchị 30)               | `src/lib/db/models/activeSyncedCatalog.ts`                                         | Ogologo oge nke ga-agafe tupu ndepụta ụdị emekọrịtara nke njikọ akwụsị ịbụ nke a pụrụ ịdabere na ya maka routing ma kwe ka registry were ọnọdụ ahụ ma ọ bụrụ na nsogbu dị (#12849). Ahịrị ndị enwetabeghị timestamp mgbe ọ bụla na-agụ dịka ndị kara ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_ANALYTICS_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `60000` (sekọnd 60)                    | `src/lib/usage/analyticsResponseCache.ts`                                          | Ogologo oge a ga-eji ọzọ nzaghachi `GET /api/usage/analytics` dị nwayọ maka otu query string ahụ (#15629). `0` na-agbanyụ cache ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ANALYTICS_CACHE_MIN_COMPUTE_MS`     | `1000` (sekọnd 1)                      | `src/lib/usage/analyticsResponseCache.ts`                                          | Naanị nzaghachi analytics ndị were opekata mpe oge a iji wuo ka a na-echekwa na cache, ka instance ndị pere mpe wee na-ahụ data ọhụrụ mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `NANOBANANA_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `120000`                               | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration.ts`                                             | Oge nchere kachasị maka ọrụ mmepụta onyonyo NanoBanana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NANOBANANA_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`                  | `2500`                                 | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration.ts`                                             | Ugboro ole a na-enyocha ọnọdụ ọrụ NanoBanana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS`           | `8000`                                 | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyUpscale.ts`                                         | Oge nkwụsị ntọala maka exponential backoff nke mgbalị izipu upscale Adobe Firefly ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `AWS_REGION`                                   | _(edobeghị)_                           | `src/lib/providers/validation.ts`, `open-sse/handlers/audioSpeech.ts`              | Region eji emepụta endpoint AWS Bedrock (Kiro, audio).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`                           | _(edobeghị)_                           | `src/lib/providers/validation.ts`, `open-sse/handlers/audioSpeech.ts`              | Nhọrọ ndabere mgbe edobeghị `AWS_REGION`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID`                        | _(edobeghị)_                           | `open-sse/executors/cloudflare-ai.ts`                                              | ID akaụntụ maka Cloudflare Workers AI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CLOUDFLARE_PLAYGROUND_CHROME_PATH`            | _(edobeghị)_                           | `open-sse/executors/cloudflare-playground.ts`                                      | Path zuru ezu nke binary Chrome desktọpụ maka executor Cloudflare AI Playground, nke a na-eji mgbe nyocha fingerprint nke headless gbochiri Chromium Playwright jikọtara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CODEBUDDY_SYSTEM_PROMPT_MAX_LEN`              | ∅ (∞)                                  | `open-sse/executors/codebuddy/shared.ts`                                           | Oke ogologo nhọrọ maka system prompt nke onye na-akpọ zitere CodeBuddy (CN na International). Na ndabara, naanị prompt nwere akara njirimara coding-agent ka a na-eji nke na-anọpụ iche dochie (content filter Tencent na-ajụ ndị ahụ); a na-edobe prompt ndị toro ogologo. Integer > 0 na-edochikwa system prompt ọ bụla toro karịa ọnụọgụ character ahụ; uru edobeghị, `0` ma ọ bụ uru na-abụghị integer na-agbanyụ oke ahụ. Ndochi ọ bụla na-edekọ iwu kpaliri ya (akara njirimara ma ọ bụ ogologo). |
| `CLOUDFLARE_API_BASE`                          | `https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4` | `src/app/api/settings/proxy/cloudflare-deploy/route.ts`                            | Dochie ntọala Cloudflare REST API nke proxy-pool Workers relay deployer na-eji (#4640 / 9router#1360).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUDFLARE_RELAY_DEFAULT_PROJECT` | `omniroute-relay`                      | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/settings/components/proxy/CloudflareRelayModal.tsx` | Aha project worker ndabara a na-atụ aro na modal "Deploy Relay" nke proxy-pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUDFLARE_RELAY_ENABLED`         | `true`                                 | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/settings/components/proxy/ProxyPoolTab.tsx`         | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji zoo nhọrọ relay Cloudflare Workers na taabụ Proxy Pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLOUDFLARED_BIN`                              | chọpụta na-akpaghị aka                 | `src/lib/cloudflaredTunnel.ts`                                                     | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary `cloudflared`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLOUDFLARED_PROTOCOL`                         | `http2`                                | `src/lib/cloudflaredTunnel.ts`                                                     | Protokọl mbufe tunnel: `http2` (ndabara), `quic`, ma ọ bụ `auto`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CLOUDFLARED_CONFIG`                           | _(edobeghị)_                           | `src/lib/cloudflaredTunnel.ts`                                                     | Ụzọ gaa na `config.yml` cloudflared a na-achịkwa na mpaghara (nke nwere `tunnel:`, `credentials-file:`, `ingress:`). Mgbe edobere ya, OmniRoute na-agba `tunnel --config <path> run` (tunnel nwere aha) kama tunnel ngwa ngwa nke na-adịru nwa oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLOUDFLARED_HOSTNAME`                         | _(site na ingress nhazi)_              | `src/lib/cloudflaredTunnel.ts`                                                     | Ntọgharị aha nnabata ọhaneze nke tunnel nwere aha (dịka `ai.example.com`), nke a na-akọ dịka `publicUrl`/`apiUrl`. Mgbe edobeghị ya, a na-agụ ya site na aha nnabata `ingress` mbụ dị na nhazi ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `DENO_DEPLOY_API_BASE`                         | `https://api.deno.com/v2`              | `src/app/api/settings/proxy/deno-deploy/route.ts`                                  | Gbanwee ntọala REST API nke Deno Deploy nke onye na-ebunye relay proxy-pool na-eji (#4643 / 9router#1437).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_DENO_RELAY_DEFAULT_PROJECT`       | `omniroute-deno-relay`                 | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/settings/components/proxy/DenoRelayModal.tsx`       | Aha ngwa Deno Deploy ndabara a na-atụ aro na modal "Deploy Relay" nke proxy-pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_DENO_RELAY_ENABLED`               | `true`                                 | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/settings/components/proxy/ProxyPoolTab.tsx`         | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji zoo nhọrọ relay Deno Deploy na taabụ Proxy Pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SEARCH_CACHE_TTL_MS`                          | `300000` (nkeji 5)                     | `open-sse/services/searchCache.ts`                                                 | TTL maka idobe nzaghachi API ọchụchọ (Perplexity, Brave, wdg.) na cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER`                | `false`                                | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                 | Gosipụta UI nnwale nke onye na-eweta ọrụ dakọtara na CC maka relay ndị bụ naanị Claude Code.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NINEROUTER_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                            | `open-sse/executors/ninerouter.ts`                                                 | Gbanwee host ebe instance 9router agbakwunyere na-ege ntị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `NINEROUTER_PORT`                              | `20130`                                | `open-sse/executors/ninerouter.ts`                                                 | Gbanwee port ebe instance 9router agbakwunyere na-ege ntị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
