| `EMBED_WS_PROXY_PORT`                          | `20131`                                | `src/lib/services/embedWsProxy.ts`                                                 | Port maka sava proxy WebSocket nke ọrụ agbakwunyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLIPROXYAPI_HOST`                             | `127.0.0.1`                            | `open-sse/executors/cliproxyapi.ts`                                                | Host njikọ CLIProxyAPI (njikọ usoro ochie).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLIPROXYAPI_PORT`                             | `5544`                                 | `open-sse/executors/cliproxyapi.ts`                                                | Port njikọ CLIProxyAPI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CLIPROXYAPI_API_KEY`                          | _(efu)_                                | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/cliproxyapiCredentials.ts`                             | Igodo ndabere nke data-plane mgbe ntọala `cliproxyapi_api_key` adịghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CLIPROXYAPI_MANAGEMENT_KEY`                   | _(efu)_                                | `src/lib/services/cliproxyAccountHealth.ts`                                        | Igodo njikwa maka ịgụ ahụike akaụntụ site na instance CLIProxyAPI a na-achịkwa n'èzí.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLIPROXYAPI_CONFIG_DIR`                       | `~/.cli-proxy-api`                     | `src/lib/versionManager/processManager.ts`                                         | Ndekọ nhazi CLIProxyAPI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLIPROXY_BIND_HOST`                           | `127.0.0.1`                            | `docker-compose.yml`                                                               | Interface host nke docker-compose na-ebipụta sidecar `cliproxyapi` na ya (#12578). Volume data ya nwere nzere OAuth/API nke ndị na-eweta ọrụ, image ahụ akpọgidere enweghịkwa ntọgharị `api-keys` data-plane dabere na env (naanị `config.yaml` etinyere), ya mere `0.0.0.0` na-ekpughe ọrụ nwere nzere nye LAN dum.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MUX_SERVICE_PORT`                             | `8322`                                 | `src/lib/services/bootstrap.ts`                                                    | Dochie ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri ebe daemon nhazi-agent Mux (coder/mux) agbakwunyere na-ege ntị (127.0.0.1 mgbe niile).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OPENWA_SERVICE_PORT`                          | `8323`                                 | `src/lib/services/bootstrap.ts`                                                    | Dochie ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri ebe daemon open-wa (akpaaka WhatsApp Web) agbakwunyere na-ege ntị (127.0.0.1 mgbe niile).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `DARIO_HOST`                                   | `127.0.0.1`                            | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Host njikọ/ịjikọta ọrụ Dario agbakwunyere (loopback naanị na ndabara).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `DARIO_PORT`                                   | `3456`                                 | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri ọrụ Dario agbakwunyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `DARIO_HOST`                                   | `127.0.0.1`                            | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Host njikọ/ịjikọta ọrụ Dario agbakwunyere (loopback naanị na ndabara).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `DARIO_PORT`                                   | `3456`                                 | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri ọrụ Dario agbakwunyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `LOCAL_HOSTNAMES`                              | _(efu)_                                | `open-sse/config/providerRegistry.ts`                                              | Aha host ndị ọzọ e ji rikoma kewaa nke a na-ewere dị ka nke "mpaghara" (aha ọrụ Docker, wdg.).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

`ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` bụ naanị maka relay ndị ọzọ na-abụghị nke mbụ nke na-anabata ndị ahịa Claude Code
naanị. OmniRoute na-edegharị arịrịọ ka relay ndị ahụ nwee ike ịnabata ha. Ọ bụrụ naanị na ịchọrọ iji
Claude Code CLI, ma ọ bụ na ị maghị ihe relay ndị a bụ, hapụ nke a ka ọ bụrụ nke agbanyụrụ ma tinye provider
kwekọrọ na Anthropic kama.

---

## 21. Ahụike Proxy

| Ihe Mgbanwe                                       | Ndabara                  | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                                                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROXY_FAST_FAIL_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                                | Oge ngwụcha maka nyocha ahụike ọdịda-ngwa-ngwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_LATENCY_WINDOW_HOURS`                      | `3`                      | `src/lib/db/proxies.ts`                                                                                 | Oghere oge (awa) maka ịgbakọ nkezi latency nke proxy ndị a na-atụle n'ime atụmatụ pool e mere ka latency dị mma.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS`                       | `30000`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                                | TTL cache nke nsonaazụ nyocha ahụike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_UNHEALTHY_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                                | TTL cache maka nyocha ahụike proxy ndị dara. Mee ka nke a dị mkpụmkpụ karịa `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS` ka e nwee ike ịnwale oge ngwụcha proxy nwa oge ọzọ ngwa ngwa mgbe concurrency dị elu, na-enweghị gbanyụọ ọdịda-ngwa-ngwa maka proxy ndị nwụrụ anwụ n'ezie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_ENABLED`                            | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                      | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji gbanyụọ scheduler ahụike proxy na-arụ n'azụ nke na-enyocha proxy ndị e debanyere aha site n'oge ruo n'oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS`                        | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                      | Etiti oge sweep nke scheduler ahụike na-arụ n'azụ na ms (opekata mpe `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS`                  | `21600000`               | `src/lib/proxyHealth/relayCadence.ts`                                                                   | Oge etiti nyocha na ms maka proxy edge-relay (`deno`/`vercel`/`cloudflare`), nke nyocha ha na-akpọte serverless isolate a na-akwụ ụgwọ (nke kacha nta bụ `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`               | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                      | Oge etiti usoro mgbake ndabere na ms: ugboro ole a na-enyocha proxy ndị ahụ na-adịghị mma na mbụ ọzọ ka proxy gbakerela wee laghachi n'usoro ntụgharị n'ebughị ụzọ malitegharị. Uru ndị dị n'okpuru `60000` ga-alaghachi na uru ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL`                           | `https://httpbin.org/ip` | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                                    | Ebe nyocha nnweta nke scheduler na endpoint `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` na-eji. Tụọ ya aka na URL dị n'ime/nke a na-akwado n'onwe gị iji zere nke ọha bụ ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_CONCURRENCY`                   | `10`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                                    | Ọnụ ọgụgụ nyocha a na-amalite n'otu oge n'ogbe ọ bụla, nke scheduler na endpoint `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` na-ekekọrịta. A na-eme ka opekempe ya bụrụ 1, a na-amachikwa ya na 50.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_STAGGER_MS`                    | `100`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                                    | Oge nchere na ms n'etiti ọpụpụ nyocha abụọ n'ime otu ogbe. Na-enweghị ya, ogbe niile na-apụ n'otu oge, IP ọpụpụ ha na-ekekọrịta nwekwara ike ime ka ebe nyocha nwere mmachi ọnụego kwụsị arịrịọ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ oghere oge ahụ; a machiri ya na 5000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_USE_PROVIDER_TARGET`                | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/providerProbeTarget.ts`                                                            | Tọọ "false" iji kwụsị inyocha ezigbo host nke provider e kenyere proxy (`GET /models`, enweghị API key) ma jiri `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL` mgbe niile kama ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_AUTO_DEACTIVATE`                    | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/statusPolicy.ts`                                                                   | Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (ndabara), nyocha nnweta akpaaka (scheduler + bọtịnụ "Nwalee Ha Niile" nke `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test`) bụ **naanị-maka-ịgụ** ma ọ dịghị mgbe ha na-ede status proxy — ọ bụ naanị onye njikwa na-ahọpụta active/inactive, ka nyocha na-adịghị akwụsi ike ghara ịhapụ proxy e kenyere n'enweghị ọrụ (#6246). Tọọ `true` iji weghachi omume ochie nke ịnwale-ma-tọọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `FLUSH_EMPTY_RETRY_ENABLED`                       | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                                      | Feature flag a na-ahọrọ ịgbanye (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala nnọchi sitere na DB dashboard ga-emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-anwalegharị ntụgharị streaming efu site n'ụzọ credential nkịtị (ruo nnwalegharị `STREAM_RECOVERY.EMPTY_TURN_RETRY_MAX`) kama igosipụta 200 efu ma ọ bụ 502 nwere ọdịnaya efu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_POOL_EGRESS_OBSERVATION`                   | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                                      | Feature flag a na-ahọrọ ịgbanye (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala nnọchi sitere na DB dashboard ga-emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-egosi nlele ọpụpụ pool nke bụ naanị-maka-ịgụ n'okpuru proxy pool na dashboard (IP ọpụpụ dị iche iche, njikọ, na nke a hụrụ ọtụtụ ugboro n'azụ otu IP n'ime awa 24 gara aga, sitere na log proxy). A naghị eji ya eme routing ma ọlị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_ENABLED`                   | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                                      | Akara njirimara a na-ahọrọ ịgbalite (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); mgbanwe e mere na dashboard DB ga-emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-anabata adreesị ndị e hụrụ nwere ụbọchị nke onye ọrụ zipụrụ maka onye ọ bụla nọ na pool ma jikọta ha na ihe a gụrụ na journal maka ngosipụta na nhazi pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_WINDOW_HOURS`              | `24`                     | `src/lib/db/proxyOperatorEgress.ts`                                                                     | Oghere oge nlele n'ime awa maka ahịrị egress ndị onye ọrụ zipụrụ (a na-agụ ya n'oge ọgụgụ ọ bụla; ịmalitegharị adịghị mkpa). E debere oke ya na 1..168; uru karịrị oke, na-abụghị ọnụọgụ, ma ọ bụ nke tọgbọ chakoo ga-alaghachi na 24 yana otu ịdọ aka ná ntị maka usoro niile, ọ dịghị mgbe ọ ga-atụfu njehie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`                               | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                      | Tọọ `true` ka scheduler nwee ike iwepụ proxy na-akpaghị aka mgbe ọdịda sochiri onwe ya ugboro ugboro.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`                         | `3`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                      | Ọnụọgụ ọdịda na-aga n'ihu tupu scheduler ewepụ proxy na-akpaghị aka (mgbe `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROXY_AUTO_DISABLE`                              | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                      | Tọọ `true` ka scheduler jiri nwayọ gbanyụọ proxy (ọnọdụ `dead`, agaghị ehichapụ ya) mgbe ọdịda sochiri onwe ya ugboro ugboro, kama iwepụ ya. Nke a bụ nhọrọ na-anaghị ebibi ihe maka `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`: proxy ahụ ga-apụ ozugbo na mkpebi pool/rotation (nyo alive-status nke mkpebi scope-pool na-eji ewepụlarị ya), a ga-emegharịkwa ya na-akpaghị aka ozugbo ọ malitere ịgafe nyocha ọzọ. Ọ na-eji otu oke `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`. Ọ bụrụ na akara abụọ ahụ bụ `true`, `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE` ga-emeri.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_PASSIVE_SKIP`                       | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                      | Nhọrọ ịwụfe n'enweghị nyocha ozugbo: mgbe ọ bụ `true`, scheduler na-awụfe nyocha live maka endpoint nke okporo ụzọ mmepụta ya na nso nso a gosilarị na ọ dị mma (opekata mpe otu ihe gara nke ọma na enweghị ọdịda proxy e kenyere ya n'ime oghere oge ahụ, ma hụ naanị otu provider). A na-agbanyụ ya na ndabara: a na-enyocha proxy ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROXY_PASSIVE_WINDOW_MS`                         | `300000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/passiveVerdict.ts`                                                                 | Oghere oge n'ime ms nke okporo ụzọ mmepụta nke passive skip na-eleba anya na ya (oke ya bụ `60000..599999`). Ọ dị mkpụmkpụ karịa nkeji iri nke etiti sweep ka ọdịda mere na nso nso a wee nwee mmetụta karịa kama ịbụ nke a gwakọtara wee daa mbà.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTROL_PLANE_PROXY_DIRECT_FALLBACK`   | `false`                  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                                        | Nye OAuth na usoro nkwado provider ohere ịgafe proxy a kpọgidere ma jikọọ ozugbo mgbe nyocha mbụ nke ohere iru proxy dara. Usoro mkpa dị irè bụ mgbanwe Feature Flags DB > env var > ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_WAIT_MS`                          | `30000` (30s)            | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts`                                                                        | Atụmatụ oge ndabara maka **ichere n'ahịrị**: ogologo oge arịrịọ nwere ike ichere oghere onye na-eweta ọrụ ma nọrọ n'ahịrị tupu a jụ ya, n'erughịkwa sistemụ upstream. A na-akwụsị ngụ oge ahụ ozugbo ọrụ malitere ịrụ — `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS` na-amachi oge mmezu ya iche. Env na-edobe naanị ndabara: `resilienceSettings.requestQueue.maxWaitMs` echekwara, ma mesịa `rateLimitOverrides.maxWaitMs` nke njikọ ọ bụla; nke ọ bụla n'ime ha na-aka ya mkpa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`                | `600000` (nkeji 10)      | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                                 | Oke ogologo oge arịrịọ a nabatara nwere ike ịnọ na mmezu tupu ndokwa rate-limit ya kubie ume — e kewapụrụ ya na atụmatụ oge ichere n'ahịrị ka mmalite fetch ji nwayọọ na gateway ndị na-abụghị incremental ghara ịgafe oge (#12027).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_QUEUE_DEPTH`                      | `0` (agbanyụrụ)          | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                                 | Oke nnabata n'ahịrị: jiri 429 `queue_full` jụ arịrịọ mgbe ọnụọgụ arịrịọ a nọlarị n'ahịrị ruru nke a. `0` = enweghị oke (ndabara).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `RATE_LIMIT_AUTO_ENABLE`                          | _(edoghị)_               | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                                 | Manye ka nchekwa rate limit nke na-agbanye onwe ya gbanye/gbanyụọ n'agbanyeghị ntọala Dashboard echekwara. Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on` iji manye mgbanye, yana `false`/`0`/`off` iji manye mgbanyụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`                       | _(edoghị → agbanyụrụ)_   | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                                          | Nsochikwa oge izuike zuru ụwa ọnụ nke onye na-eweta ọrụ/njikọ gafee arịrịọ dị iche iche, nke a ga-eji aka họrọ ịbanye na ya. Ọ na-agbanyụ na ndabara (ọ na-ekpuchi otu mpaghara na Connection Cooldown / Provider Circuit Breaker). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on` iji gbanye ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MIN_MS`                        | `5000`                   | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                                          | Oge izuike kacha nta (ms) tupu a nwaa onye na-eweta ọrụ/njikọ dara ada ọzọ. Ọ na-abawanye n'ụdị exponential dịka ọdịda ndị na-esochi ibe ha si dị. A na-eji ya naanị mgbe `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED` dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MAX_MS`                        | `300000` (nkeji 5)       | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                                          | Oke kachasị nke oge izuike (ms) tupu a nwaa onye na-eweta ọrụ/njikọ dara ada ọzọ n'agbanyeghị ihe ọ bụla. A na-eji ya naanị mgbe `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED` dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`                         | _(edoghị → agbanyụrụ)_   | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (mkpụrụ ntọala) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (usoro ọrụ)    | **Ihe ọ bụ:** iweghachi stream upstream e bepụrụ n'ụzọ na-apụtaghị ìhè (port nke free-claude-code). Ọ na-ejide windo SSE mmeghe ruo `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS` (750 ms), ka mbepụ _tupu commit_ — nke na-eme tupu byte ọ bụla erute onye ahịa — nwee ike imeghegharị ma nwalee ọzọ n'ụzọ onye ahịa na-agaghị ahụ. **Mgbe a ga-agbanye ya:** mgbe upstream anaghị akwụsi ike/na-ebipụkarị stream na byte 0 mgbe stream na-amalite; hapụ ya ka ọ GBANYỤRỤ ma ọ bụrụ na ị gaghị anagide mgbakwunye oge ruru 750 ms tupu token mbụ apụta na stream ọ bụla. Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on`. Ọ na-akụ mkpụrụ ntọala Resilience echekwara; ntọala Dashboard na-aka ya mkpa ozugbo edobere ya. |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_MIDSTREAM_ENABLED`               | _(edoghị → gbanyụọ)_     | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (mkpụrụ mbido) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (usoro arụmọrụ) | **Gịnị:** ịga n’ihu n’etiti iyi (Agba 4.4) — mgbe mbelata _post-commit_ gasịrị (bytes erutelarị onye ahịa), jiri ederede ezughị ezu dịka assistant prefill rịọ arịrịọ ọzọ ma jikọta mgbakwunye fọdụrụ. Ọ bụ naanị maka iyi ederede nkịtị dakọtara na OpenAI; ọ naghị arụ ọrụ ma ọ bụrụ na oku ngwá ọrụ ka na-aga. **Mgbe a ga-agbanye ya:** maka mmepụta ogologo ndị a na-akwụsị n’etiti azịza, ma ị nabatara ka ọdụ azịza eweghachiri abịa otu mgbe kama ịbịa token n’otu n’otu. Ọ dabereghị na `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED` (profaịlụ ihe ize ndụ dị iche). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on`.                                                                                                |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_ENABLED`              | _(edoghị → gbanyụọ)_     | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` → `open-sse/services/throughputWatchdog.ts`                            | Ihe nlekota ọsọ mmepụta bara uru nke iyi na-arụ ọrụ, nke a na-ahọrọ ịgbanye. Ọ na-achọpụta iyi ndị na-aga n’ihu izipu chunks mana na-anọ n’okpuru ọsọ mmepụta assistant ahaziri; heartbeats, usage events, deltas efu, na usoro ngwá ọrụ/echiche anaghị eme ka à ga-asị na e nwere ọganihu. Ọ dị iche na oge ngwụcha n’ihi enweghị ọrụ na oge ngwụcha siri ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WARMUP_MS`            | `30000`                  | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                              | Oge nnabata tupu nyocha ọsọ mmepụta amalite, nke oke ya bụ 0–600000 ms.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WINDOW_MS`            | `30000`                  | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                              | Windo mmepụta bara uru na-agagharị, nke oke ya bụ 1000–600000 ms; a chọrọ otu windo zuru ezu tupu ịkwụsị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_BYTES_PER_SECOND` | `4`                      | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                              | Ọsọ bytes kacha nta kwa sekọnd maka mmepụta assistant UTF-8 (nnọchi token e ji akpachapụ anya), nke oke ya bụ 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_USEFUL_BYTES`     | `1`                      | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                              | Nlele mmepụta bara uru kacha nta na-abụghị efu nke a na-ewere dị ka ihe a pụrụ ịtụ, nke oke ya bụ bytes 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS`                          | `3000`                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                           | Etiti oge nkewa (ms) n’etiti nyocha ahụike token nke ndị na-eweta ọrụ mgbe usoro na-amalite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MIN_MS`                       | `500`                    | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                           | Jitter enweghị usoro kacha nta (ms) a na-agbakwunye n’elu `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` n’etiti nyocha ahụike token nke ndị na-eweta ọrụ, iji gbochie ibu arịrịọ ịgbawa n’otu oge (Nsogbu #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MAX_MS`                       | `5000`                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                           | Jitter enweghị usoro kacha ukwuu (ms) a na-agbakwunye n’elu `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` n’etiti nyocha ahụike token nke ndị na-eweta ọrụ, iji gbochie ibu arịrịọ ịgbawa n’otu oge (Nsogbu #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `HEALTHCHECK_BATCH_SIZE`                          | `20`                     | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                           | Ogo batch nke nyocha ndị na-aga n’otu oge maka nyocha ahụike token niile mgbe usoro na-amalite; uru ndị ka ukwuu na-enyocha njikọ ndị ọzọ n’otu oge, uru ndị ka nta na-ebelata ibu arịrịọ mberede (Nsogbu #7875, nlọghachi mmejọ nke #7719).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `REQUEST_RETRY`                                   | `2`                      | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                                | Ọnụọgụ nnwale ọzọ akpaka a ga-eme mgbe nzaghachi cooldown metụtara otu model pụtara, tupu iweghachiri onye ahịa njehie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `MAX_RETRY_INTERVAL_SEC`                          | `30`                     | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                                | Oke kachasị nke oge ndaghachi azụ (sekọnd) n’etiti mnwalegharị cooldown. A na-amachibido ya n’uru a n’agbanyeghị `Retry-After` sitere n’elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `HEADROOM_URL`                                    | `http://localhost:8787`  | `src/lib/headroom/detect.ts`                                                                            | URL proxy Headroom nke na-echekwa token. Usoro ndụ dashboard (`api/headroom/*`) na-ebido CLI `headroom-ai` mpaghara na loopback na ndabara; gbanwee ya naanị ma ọ bụrụ na ịchọrọ ka ọ rụtụ aka na proxy Docker sidecar dị na mpụga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

### Constants nhazi mgbake stream

A na-ahazi omume njigide mgbake site na constants e dere kpọmkwem n’ime
`open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`), nke egosiri ebe a maka ntụaka —
ịgbanwe ha chọrọ idezi koodu, ọ bụghị env var:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` — ogologo oge a na-ejide windo SSE mmeghe
  ka e nwee ike ịnwalegharị nkwụsị mere n’oge tupu e nyefee byte ọ bụla n’aka client.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` — oke siri ike maka windo a na-ejide; mee commit
  (flush + passthrough) ozugbo byte ruru ọnụọgụ a, n’agbanyeghị timer.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` — oke kachasị nke mmepegharị upstream
  stream n’enweghị mmetụta pụtara ìhè mgbe holdback ka emebeghị commit.

> **Oke ọnụego sliding-window maka provider ọ bụla (enweghị env var):** _fallback_
> nke oke ọnụego sliding-window maka provider ọ bụla e si FCC bufee dị na koodu
> (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, nke ejikọrọ site na
> `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) mana ọ na-abịa na **map ndabara efu**
> ma **enweghị env var maka operator** ugbu a — a na-eme ka ọ rụọ ọrụ naanị site na test hook /
> ndezi koodu. E ji ụma hapụ ya n’ime tebụl dị n’elu. Relay limiter maka `(token, IP)`
> nke _nwere_ knob bụ `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Netwọkụ & Ports).

## 22. Nchọpụta na ndozi nsogbu

> [!CAUTION]
> Ngbanwe ndị a na-emepụta **mmepụta zuru ezu** ma nwee ike ikpughe data dị nzuzo. **Agbanyela ha mgbe ọ bụla na gburugburu mmepụta.**

| Ngbanwe                               | Ndabara                                    | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CURSOR_DEBUG`                        | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Tọọ `1` iji gbanye ndekọ zuru ezu nke Cursor executor (iberibe SSE a tụgharịrị, wdg.).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG`                 | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Aha ọzọ nke `CURSOR_DEBUG` nke dakọtara na nsụgharị ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CURSOR_DUMP_FILE`                    | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ụzọ faịlụ nhọrọ nke na-anata iberibe Cursor raw a tụgharịrị mgbe `CURSOR_DEBUG=1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                                   | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Oge njedebe nke stream mgbe ọ naghị arụ ọrụ (ms) maka Cursor executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CURSOR_KV_GRACE_MS`                  | `2000`                                     | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Oge mgbaghara (ms) mgbe composer kv_after_text soft terminator gasịrị ma ọ bụrụ na byte ka dị na buffer — nke a na-enye oku ngwaọrụ exec_mcp nke na-esote oge iji mezue frame ya.                                                                                                                                             |
| `CURSOR_TOOL_DIRECTIVE`               | agbanyere (`!== "0"`)                      | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ntuziaka tool-commit nke na-eme ka composer-2.5 kpọọ ngwaọrụ n'ụzọ a pụrụ ịdabere na ya. Tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION_APPEND` | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/translator/request/claude-to-openai.ts`, `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts` | Ederede system prompt nke onye ọrụ sistemụ kọwara, nke a na-agbakwunye na system message MGBE ntụgharị gasịrị (ntinye mgbe ntụgharị gasịrị), ma rute n'ụzọ codex/Responses na `/v1/messages`. A na-ejikwa ya dị ka mbido ntuziaka a na-ewepụ na ngọngọ system preamble ndị e weghachiri. Hapụ ya ka edoghị ya iji gbanyụọ ya. |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRIP_SYSTEM_PREAMBLE`     | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                            | `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts`, `open-sse/utils/directivePreambleStripper.ts`     | Tọọ `1` iji wepụ ngọngọ system-prompt preamble ndị e weghachiri site na mmalite nke stream openai→claude. Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara — usoro nyocha ndị ahụ dabere n'ụdị ederede Bekee ma na-agbanwe payload nzaghachi, ya mere nzaghachi nke malitere n'ụzọ ziri ezi site na ngalaba dị otu ahụ ga-efunahụ ya.                  |
| `CURSOR_IMAGE_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `15000`                                    | `open-sse/utils/cursorImages.ts`                                                                      | Oge njedebe nbudata maka onyonyo ọ bụla (ms) maka ntinye ọhụhụ `image_url` dị n'ime ime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Mgbe edoghị ya, handler na-eji `providerSpecificData.agentBin`, mesịa PATH.                                                                                                                                                                                          |

## 23. Njikọta GitHub

Kwe ka ndị ọrụ nwee ike ịkọ nsogbu ozugbo site na Dashboard.

| Ihe Mgbanwe           | Ndabara      | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------------- | ------------ | ------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_REPO`  | _(edobeghị)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`           | Ebe nchekwa n'ụdị `owner/repo`.                                                                                                                    |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(edobeghị)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`           | GitHub Personal Access Token nwere ikike `issues:write`.                                                                                           |
| `GITHUB_TOKEN`        | _(edobeghị)_ | nhazi mbido nke nsogbu / ihe enyemaka cloud agent | Token nnweta GitHub izugbe a na-eji dị ka ndabere maka `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN`, nke ihe enyemaka cloud agent dị na `src/lib/cloudAgent/*` na-ejikwa. |

---

## Ọnọdụ Mbuga

Maka ntuziaka SRE nke backend relay (omume ts/bifrost/auto, ebe a ga-edowe 9router ma e jiri ya tụnyere CLIProxyAPI, na atụmatụ ndabere maka oke ọsọ mbufe), lee [Atụmatụ Backend Relay](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Mmepe Obodo Dịkarịsịrị Mfe

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Mmepụta Docker

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<emepụtara>
API_KEY_SECRET=<emepụtara>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<emepụtara>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<emepụtara>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Enweghị Njikọ Ịntanetị / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS nwere Proxy Ntụgharị (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<emepụtara>
API_KEY_SECRET=<emepụtara>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<emepụtara>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

---

## 24. Ebe Nnwale Nchekwa Skills (v3.8.0+)

Oke na ntọala nchekwa ndị a na-etinye mgbe framework Skills (`src/lib/skills/`) na-eme automation ndị ọrụ kọwara n'ime gburugburu e kewapụrụ maka nnwale.

| Ihe Mgbanwe                       | Ndabara                                               | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| --------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (10 s)                                        | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke oge wall-clock maka oge mmejuputa nke ọ bụla nke koodu skill dị na sandbox. Nke a bụ oke siri ike; a ga-akwụsị ihe ọ bụla gafere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(na-eji `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`` dị ka ndabere)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Oke oge maka nhazi mmejuputa skill n'ogo dị elu. Tọọ ya karịa `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` iji kwe ka usoro ọrụ nwere ọtụtụ nzọụkwụ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                                      | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ọnụ byte kachasị skill nwere ike ịgụ site na otu faịlụ ọ bụla dị na sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                                     | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ọnụ byte kachasị a ga-edekọ site na otu nzaghachi HTTP ọ bụla n'ime skill.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                              | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke siri ike nke mkpụrụedemede stdout/stderr a na-eweghachi site na otu ọkpụkpọ sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                               | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Tọọ `1`/`true` iji kwe ka njikọ netwọk na-apụ apụ site n'ime sandbox. Ndabara ya bụ **nkewapụrụ** maka nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(efu)_                                               | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ndepụta onyonyo container ndị e kwere maka mmejuputa sandbox, nke rikoma kewara. Ọ bụrụ na ọ tọgbọ chakoo, ọ pụtara na ọ bụ naanị ndabara arụnyere n'ime ka a ga-eji.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(ndabara arụnyere n'ime)_                            | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Onyonyo container a na-eji mgbe a na-emepụta sandbox Docker na-akwado. Gbanwee ya iji kpọchie onyonyo ntọala omenala e wusiri ike.                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                                | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Runtime container maka idobe skill na sandbox: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` na-ahọrọ runtime arụnyere kacha mma dabere na OS nke host (Apple Container/OrbStack na macOS, WSL Container na Windows, Podman na Linux), ma na-eji Docker dị ka ndabere ma ọ bụrụ na ndị ahụ adịghị. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Ime ka `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` rụọ ọrụ na-emepe ụzọ egress site na koodu skill ọ bụla. Jikọtara ya na `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` yana amụma `CORS_ORIGIN`/proxy siri ike n'ebe mbugote ọtụtụ ndị na-eji ọnụ.
