## 25. Oke Ojiji Ndị Na-eweta Ọrụ, Ọwara Netwọkụ, Nkwado Ndabere & Ihe Runtime Ndị Ọzọ

Endpoint oke ojiji ndị na-eweta ọrụ, ọwara netwọkụ (Tailscale, Ngrok, proxy ndozi MITM), ọdọ egress 1Proxy, nkwado ndabere nchekwa data na obere mgbanwe ndị akọwapụtara maka atụmatụ ọ bụla nke layer executor ma ọ bụ script na-eji.

| Mgbanwe                                        | Ndabara                                                                     | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Eriri njikọ Redis maka backend nke ihe mgbochi ọsọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_HOST`                     | _(host mmepụta)_                                                            | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Gbanwee host eji enweta oke coding-plan Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_QUOTA_URL`                | ewepụtara site na host                                                      | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Mgbanwe URL zuru ezu nke oke maka Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_COOKIE`                            | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Kuki nnọkọ console maka ụzọ mbata oke Token Plan nkeonwe nke Qwen Cloud / Model Studio (igodo API inference enweghị ike ịgụ ya). Detuo header arịrịọ `Cookie` niile — ọ nwere `login_qwencloud_ticket` — site na oku `api.json` ọ bụla gaa na `cs-data.qwencloud.com` na home.qwencloud.com › Ịgba Ụgwọ › Ndebanye Aha (F12 › Netwọkụ). Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo ma bụrụ naanị maka nnọkọ ahụ; ọ ka mma iji oghere Dashboard `qwenCloudCookie` nke njikọ ọ bụla.                                                                                                       |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_SEC_TOKEN`                         | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Mgbanwe `sec_token` ejiri aka tinye maka ụzọ mbata console Token Plan. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; mgbe edobeghị ya, fetcher na-eji kuki ahụ chọpụta ya site na HTML dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_HOST`                         | `https://cs-data.qwencloud.com`                                             | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Mgbanwe host nke ụzọ mbata maka fetcher oke Token Plan nkeonwe (dịka ọmụmaatụ, `bailian-singapore-cs.alibabacloud.com` maka console Model Studio).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_DASHBOARD_URL`                | `https://home.qwencloud.com/`                                               | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | URL dashboard eji achọpụta `sec_token` site na HTML onye ọrụ banyere na ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `XIAOMI_MIMO_CONSOLE_COOKIE`                   | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/xiaomi-mimo.ts`                                  | Kuki nnọkọ console maka API oke Token Plan Xiaomi MiMo (`platform.xiaomimimo.com/api/v1/tokenPlan/usage` + `tokenPlan/detail` — igodo API inference enweghị ike ịgụ ya). Detuo header arịrịọ `Cookie` niile — ọ nwere `api-platform_serviceToken` na `userId` — site na arịrịọ ọ bụla gaa na `platform.xiaomimimo.com` na ibe Token Plan (F12 › Netwọkụ). Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo ma bụrụ naanị maka nnọkọ ahụ; ọ ka mma iji oghere Dashboard `xiaomiMimoConsoleCookie` nke njikọ ọ bụla.                                                                             |
| `XIAOMI_MIMO_MONTHLY_TOKEN_LIMIT`              | `4100000000`                                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/xiaomi-mimo.ts`                                  | Mmefu ego kwa ọnwa (Credits) maka Xiaomi MiMo ndabere nke OmniRoute na-esochi, nke a na-eji mgbe ahazighị kuki console. Ndabara ya bụ 4.1B nke ọkwa Lite; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ Credits nke atụmatụ ahụ (Standard 11B / Pro 38B / Max 82B). Ntọgharị `providerSpecificData.monthlyTokenLimit` nke njikọ ọ bụla na-ebute ụzọ karịa env var a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_VISION_FE_PATH`             | `/costing-balance/free-quota-image-video`                                   | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Ntọgharị ụzọ front-end nke console maka iwepụta oke vision/media nke ọkwa efu Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_MULTIMODAL_FE_PATH`         | `/costing-balance/free-quota-multimodal`                                    | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Ntọgharị ụzọ front-end nke console maka iwepụta oke multimodal nke ọkwa efu Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_AUDIO_FE_PATH`              | `/costing-balance/free-quota-audio`                                         | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Ntọgharị ụzọ front-end nke console maka iwepụta oke audio nke ọkwa efu Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_ALLOWLIST_PATH`             | _(edoghị)_                                                                  | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierAllowlist.ts`                           | Ụzọ nhọrọ gaa na ntọgharị JSON mpaghara maka allowlist ụdịdị ederede nke ọkwa efu Alibaba arụnyere n'ime ya. Ọ bụrụ na nke ahụ adịghị, ọ na-eji `$DATA_DIR/alibaba-free-tier-allowlist.json`, ma mesịa `config/alibaba-free-tier-allowlist.json`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CONTEXT_RESERVE_TOKENS`                       | `1024`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/contextManager.ts`                                     | Token ndị edobere maka mmepụta mmecha mgbe a na-agbakọ mmefu prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CONTEXT_KEEP_LATEST_IMAGES`                   | `2`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/contextManager.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ onyonyo inline kachasị ọhụrụ a ga-edobe mgbe a na-ewepụ ndị ochie iji daba na windo context (#8560).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MODEL_ALIAS_COMPAT_ENABLED`                   | agbanyere                                                                   | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                                              | Gbanye ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ oyi akwa ndakọrịta model-alias ochie nke ndị ahịa ochie na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_EMERGENCY_FALLBACK`                 | agbanyere                                                                   | `open-sse/services/emergencyFallback.ts`                                  | Tọọ `false` (ma ọ bụ `0`) iji gbanyụọ ndabere mberede maka mmefu ego agwụla, nke na-atụgharị arịrịọ dara ada gaa na ụdịdị `nvidia`/`openai/gpt-oss-120b` efu. Usoro ibute ụzọ dị irè bụ ntọgharị Feature Flags DB > env var > ndabara; ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị, ọrụ ahụ na-eji uru env nke mbụ dịka ndabere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `COMMAND_CODE_CALLBACK_PORT`                   | _(edoghị)_                                                                  | `src/app/api/providers/command-code/auth/shared.ts`                       | Port mpaghara a na-eji maka callback ụdị OAuth sitere na onye enyemaka Command Code CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `COMMAND_CODE_VERSION`                         | `0.33.2`                                                                    | `open-sse/executors/commandCode.ts`                                       | Uru a na-eziga dịka nkụnyeisi `x-command-code-version` gaa na Command Code upstream. Gbanwee ya iji bulie ụdị CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `COMMANDCODE_API_URL`                          | `https://api.commandcode.ai`                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/command-code.ts`                                 | URL ntọala maka Command Code usage/quota upstream nke smartphone quota-fetcher telemetry na-eji. Gbanwee ya maka Command Code API nke ị na-akwado n'onwe gị/ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MITM_LOCAL_PORT`                              | `443`                                                                       | `src/mitm/server.cjs`                                                     | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri njikọ mpaghara maka MITM debug proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MITM_DISABLE_TLS_VERIFY`                      | `0`                                                                         | `src/mitm/server.cjs`                                                     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji gbanyụọ nkwenye TLS nke upstream (maka mmepe naanị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `MITM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | `60000`                                                                     | `src/mitm/socketTimeouts.ts`, `src/mitm/server.cjs`                       | Oge ngafe (ms) nke socket na-adịghị arụ ọrụ maka njikọ ndị proxy na-ebufe; a na-akwụsị socket ndị nọgidere na-enweghị ọrụ gafee oge a iji zere ịhapụ tunnel ndị meghere ọkara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `BRIDGE_PORT`                                  | `20129`                                                                     | `bin/antigravity-bridge.mjs`                                              | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri nke àkwà mmiri Antigravity MITM na-ege ntị na ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ROUTER_URL`                                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/v1/antigravity`                                     | `bin/antigravity-bridge.mjs`                                              | Ebe njedebe router nke àkwà mmiri ahụ na-ebuga okporoụzọ Antigravity na ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CERT_DIR`                                     | `<DATA_DIR>/mitm`                                                           | `bin/antigravity-bridge.mjs`                                              | Na-edochi ndekọ ebe àkwà mmiri ahụ na-agụ ihe TLS ya. A na-ahọrọ ụdị cert ahụ dịka sava AgentBridge si eme: `server.crt`/`server.key` maka nrụnye legacy leaf, ma ọ bụghị ya `ca.crt`/`ca.key` (root CA, otu leaf maka host ọ bụla e jidere; `MITM_ROOT_CA_ENABLED=true` na-amanye ya). Àkwà mmiri ahụ na-apụ mgbe ọ na-amalite ma ọ bụrụ na faịlụ maka ụdị na-arụ ọrụ adịghị.                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MITM_VERBOSE`                                 | `1`                                                                         | `src/mitm/server.cjs`, `src/mitm/_internal/bypass.cjs`                    | Ogo nkọwa ndekọ mkpebi routing: `0` na-emechi ya ọnụ, uru ndị dị elu na-edekọ mkpebi bypass/route karịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MITM_ROOT_CA_ENABLED`                         | `false`                                                                     | `src/mitm/manager.ts`                                                     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji họrọ ụdị cert root-CA + per-host-leaf (#6684). Nrụnye ọhụrụ na-enweta ya na-akpaghị aka; nrụnye nwere legacy leaf a tụkwasịrị obi nke dịbu na-edobe cert fixed-SAN legacy ma ọ bụrụ na a họrọghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MITM_CERT_MODE`                               | `legacy`                                                                    | `src/mitm/manager.ts`, `src/mitm/server.cjs`                              | Onye njikwa MITM na-edobe ya MAKA usoro proxy emepụtara (`root-ca` \| `legacy`) — ọ na-egosipụta mkpebi mbugharị cert; e kwesịghị iji aka tọọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_SUDO`                            | `0`                                                                         | `src/mitm/systemCommands.ts`                                              | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` (truthy) iji wepụ `sudo` dị n'ihu n'iwu MITM cert-trust — maka mbunye na-enweghị root / nke kewara site na namespace onye ọrụ, ebe onye njikwa ji aka ya tụkwasịrị CA obi (dịka ọmụmaatụ, site na usoro extra-CA-certs nke Node).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SKIP_ANTIGRAVITY_DNS`                         | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `src/mitm/dns/provision.ts`                                               | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji mafere kpamkpam imepụta ndekọ DNS `/etc/hosts` maka aha nnabata proxy Antigravity — maka containers na-enweghị sudo/root.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DNS_WRITE`                     | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `src/mitm/dns/dnsConfig.ts`                                               | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji mafere ide ihe na faịlụ hosts mgbe a na-agbakwunye/na-ewepụ ndekọ DNS — maka gburugburu nnwale sandboxed ma ọ bụ nke bụ naanị ọgụgụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_SYSTEM_TRUST`                  | `0`                                                                         | `src/mitm/cert/install.ts`, `src/mitm/tproxy/caTrust.ts`                  | Nchedo maka nnwale/CI naanị: tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji mee ka ntinye/mwepụ ntụkwasị obi cert ghara ime ihe ọ bụla, ka usoro nnwale ghara ịgbanwe ebe nchekwa ntụkwasị obi OS ma ọlị. Ntọala nnwale na usoro ọrụ CI na-edozi ya na-akpaghị aka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CHANGELOG_BASE_REF`                           | _(akpaka)_                                                                  | `scripts/check/check-changelog-integrity.mjs`                             | Base ref akọwapụtara nke ọma maka mgbochi anti CHANGELOG-eat (ndabara ya bụ alaka ntọala PR na CI, ma ọ bụ `release/v*` kachasị elu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `FREE_PROXY_AUTO_SYNC_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                                                     | `src/lib/freeProxyProviders/scheduler.ts`                                 | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji gbanye scheduler nke na-emekọrịta pool free-proxy na-akpaghị aka n'azụ. A ga-ahọrọ ịgbanye ya; agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

Ọ na-emetụta naanị mgbe `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND=auto`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`                           | _(edoghị)_                                                                  | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                              | Ụzọ faịlụ gaa na asambodo PEM TLS iji nye `omniroute serve` site na HTTPS (ya na `--tls-cert` bụ otu ihe). A ga-ejikọta ya na `OMNIROUTE_TLS_KEY`; sava kwụụrụ onwe ya ga-akwụsị TLS na listener ahụ (`wss://` na-arụkwa ọrụ n'enweghị mgbanwe). Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya → HTTP nkịtị. Inye naanị otu n'ime cert/key, ma ọ bụ ụzọ faịlụ a na-apụghị ịgụ, na-edekọ ịdọ aka ná ntị ma nọgide na HTTP.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TLS_KEY`                            | _(edoghị)_                                                                  | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                              | Ụzọ faịlụ gaa na private key PEM TLS maka HTTPS nke `omniroute serve` (ya na `--tls-key` bụ otu ihe). A ga-ejikọta ya na `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`. Lee `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_ENABLED`            | `0`                                                                         | `src/lib/security/localEndpoints.ts`                                      | Mgbanwe ukwu maka ụzọ `/api/local/*`. Mgbe edoghị ya ma ọ bụ mgbe ọ bụ `0`, ụzọ `/api/local/*` niile na-eweghachi 503 na production. Ọ ga-abụrịrị `1` na deployments ndị na-abụghị loopback iji mee ka Redis launcher na ndị ọzọ yiri ya maka ịmalite ọrụ local n'otu click rụọ ọrụ. Nchedo okpukpu abụọ yana nhazi route-guard `isLocalOnlyPath()` (`LOCAL_ONLY_API_PREFIXES` dị na `src/server/authz/routeGuard.ts`).                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_TOKEN`              | _(edoghị)_                                                                  | `src/lib/security/localEndpoints.ts`                                      | Bearer token maka ndị na-akpọ `/api/local/*` ndị na-anọghị na loopback (dịka ọmụmaatụ, ngwa desktop). Mgbe edobere ya, arịrịọ sitere na IP ndị na-abụghị loopback ga-eburịrị `Authorization: Bearer <token>`. A chọrọ ya mgbe `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_ENABLED=1` na deployments ndị na-abụghị loopback.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_CONTAINER_NAME`               | `omniroute-redis`                                                           | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Aha container maka ihe mbido Redis otu-pịa (`omniroute redis up`). Ma CLI ma GUI `RedisLauncherPanel` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_HOST_PORT`                    | `6379`                                                                      | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Port host maka ihe mbido Redis otu-pịa. Gbanwee ya ma ọ bụrụ na host ejirila 6379. Port ime container ahụ ka na-abụ 6379.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST`                    | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Interface host nke ihe mbido Redis otu-pịa na-ebipụta na ya. Ihe mbido ahụ na-amalite Redis NA-ENWEGHỊ okwuntughe, ya mere ijikọ ya na `0.0.0.0` na-enye host ọ bụla dị na LAN gị Redis na-enweghị nyocha njirimara — gbasaa ya naanị ma ọ bụrụ na ị tinyekwara okwuntughe n'onwe gị na instance ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Interface host nke docker-compose na-ebipụta Redis sidecar na ya (#9286). Redis nke compose na-arụ ọrụ na-enweghị `requirepass`; container ngwa na-enweta ya site na netwọkụ compose (`redis:6379`) — port e bipụtara dị naanị maka ngwaọrụ ndị dị n'akụkụ host. `0.0.0.0` na-ekpughere LAN niile Redis na-enweghị nyocha njirimara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `REDIS_PORT`                                   | `6379`                                                                      | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Port host maka Redis sidecar nke compose.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `APP_BIND_HOST`                                | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`, `docker-compose.prod.yml`                           | Interface host nke docker-compose na-ebipụta port dashboard/API/live-WS nke ngwa ahụ n'onwe ya na ya (#12568). Ebe `REQUIRE_API_KEY=false` bụ ndabara dị na `.env.example`, `0.0.0.0` na-ekpughere LAN/WAN niile proxy LLM `/v1` a na-eji n'amaghị onye ọrụ — gbasaa ya naanị mgbe `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` ma ọ bụ mgbe reverse proxy dị n'ihu na-amanye nyocha njirimara nke ya.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `QDRANT_BIND_HOST`                             | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Interface host nke docker-compose na-ebipụta Qdrant memory sidecar na ya (#12578). Otu echiche ahụ gbasara ikpughe ya na LAN dịka `REDIS_BIND_HOST`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `BIFROST_BIND_HOST`                            | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Interface host nke docker-compose na-ebipụta Bifrost router sidecar na ya (#12578). Otu echiche ahụ gbasara ikpughe ya na LAN dịka `REDIS_BIND_HOST`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `REDIS_KEY_PREFIX`                             | `omniroute:`                                                                | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Prefix namespace a na-etinye na key Redis OmniRoute ọ bụla (rate limiter, cache nyocha njirimara, ebe nchekwa quota, warmup circuit breaker). | `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`             | _(edoghị — usoro ahụ agbanyụrụ)_                                            | `src/lib/api/internalServiceAuth.ts`                                      | Ihe nzuzo a na-ekekọrịta maka nzọụkwụ REST ime nke na-echekwa njirimara (#9260): Akụkụ OmniRoute na-akpọ route OmniRoute mpaghara ndị ọzọ na-eziga ya dịka `x-omniroute-internal-service-token` ka e wee chekwaa njirimara onye kpọrọ na mbụ. Ọnụọgụ dị elu = nrụpụta dị nwayọ, ọchụchọ dị ngwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

Ọ na-enye ohere ka ihu-ngwa nlekọta (dịka ngwa desktọpụ VibeProxy) detuo iwu ntụgharị nke ya n'ime injin ịlaghachi n'akaụntụ ọzọ nke backend.                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_RATE_LIMIT_RESET_SECONDS`  | `0`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Oge nchere (sekọnd) a na-etinye n'akaụntụ e tinyere oke arịrịọ mgbe ọrụ dị n'elu enyeghị ntụziaka doro anya maka nrụgharị. `0` = jiri oge nchere ndabara nke injin kama uru edoziri iji dochie ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_DISABLE_TAG_WITHOUT_RESET` | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Oyiri nke mmasị ihu-ngwa “akpanyela akara dị ka nke e tinyere oke arịrịọ ma ọ bụrụ na enweghị oge nrụgharị”.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_429`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Na-enyere ịlaghachi n'akaụntụ ọzọ aka maka njehie ọnọdụ `429`. Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (ma `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_ENABLED=true`), `429` anaghịzi akpalite ntụgharị akaụntụ, kama a na-eweghachiri ya onye ahịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `429` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS` a chọrọ tupu a tụgharịa akaụntụ ahụ. `1` (ndabara) na-atụgharị ya ozugbo, na-echekwa omume e ji mara usoro ahụ kemgbe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Windọ na-akpụ akpụ (sekọnd) nke a na-agụ njehie `429` n'ime ya iji ruo `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_500`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Na-enyere ịlaghachi n'akaụntụ ọzọ aka maka njehie sava `5xx` (ewepụ `502`, nke nwere klaasị nke ya). Mgbe ọ bụ `false`, njehie ndị a anaghịzi akpalite ntụgharị akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `5xx` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS` a chọrọ tupu a tụgharịa akaụntụ ahụ. `1` (ndabara) na-atụgharị ya ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Windo na-amị amị (sekọnd) nke a na-agụta njehie `5xx` n'ime ya maka iru `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_502`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ntọala nkwado ndabere dabere na steeti iji mee ihe maka njehie `502` (ọnụ ụzọ na-adịghị mma). Mgbe ọ bụ `false`, `502` anaghịzi akpalite ịgbanwe akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `502` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS` a chọrọ tupu a gbanwee akaụntụ ahụ. `1` (ndabara) na-agbanwe ya ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Windo na-amị amị (sekọnd) nke a na-agụta njehie `502` n'ime ya maka iru `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400`                      | `false`                                                                     | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Nhọrọ a ga-ahọrọ iji rụọ ọrụ (ndabara bụ ỌGBANYỤ): mgbe ọ bụ `true`, `400` nkịtị (arịrịọ na-adịghị mma) na-akpalitekwa ịgbanwe akaụntụ. Nke a na-agbakwụnye omume naanị — ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-egbochi omume dị adị nke injin ahụ ebe `400` nwere ederede gbasara oke ojiji/koota ka na-agafe gaa na akaụntụ ọzọ n'agbanyeghị ọkọlọtọ a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `400` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS` a chọrọ tupu a gbanwee akaụntụ ahụ (a na-elele nke a naanị mgbe `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Windo na-amị amị (sekọnd) nke a na-agụta njehie `400` n'ime ya maka iru `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

### Onye Nhazi Nkwadebe Claude

Nkwadebe Cron na-achịkwa maka njikọ Anthropic OAuth ndị họọrọ isonye, ka obere arịrịọ ahaziri n'oge meghee windo oke ojiji nke awa 5 kama ịbụ arịrịọ izizi n'ezie ga-eme ya (#8848). Onye nhazi ahụ anaghị arụ ọrụ ma ọ bụrụ na `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` abụghị eziokwu **yana** edere njikọ ahụ na `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; ndepụta njikọ efu pụtara na ọ dịghị ihe a ga-akwadebe ọbụlagodi mgbe env var ahụ dị ọkụ.

| Agbanwe                        | Ndabara                     | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(edoghị → gbanyụrụ)_       | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Mgbanwe ukwu maka ihe nhazi warmup. Ọ na-anabata `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (ọ naghị ele mkpụrụedemede ukwu ma ọ bụ nta anya, a na-ewepụ oghere). Uru ọ bụla ọzọ, ma ọ bụ ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, ga-ahapụ ihe nhazi ahụ ka ọ gbanyụrụ. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Nkwupụta cron nwere oghere ise maka tick warmup, nke a na-enyocha na `America/Los_Angeles` (mpaghara oge nrụgharị Anthropic) n'agbanyeghị elekere host.                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Ọnụọgụ njikọ a na-eme warmup n'otu oge n'ime tick ọ bụla. A na-amachibido ya n'etiti `1`-`10`; uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ ga-alaghachi na `3`.                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Model a na-eji maka arịrịọ warmup. Gbanwee ya naanị ma ọ bụrụ na ndabara adịghị na atụmatụ gị; họrọ model kachasị ọnụ ala nke ka nwere ike imepe window ahụ.                                                                    |

### Oge VNC Maka Nbanye Ihe Nchọgharị & Utuaha Data-Dir

Chromium+VNC etinyere n'ime container nke a na-eji ejide ozi nzere site na nbanye ihe nchọgharị mmekọrịta (`/api/vnc-session`), tinyere utuaha `DATA_DIR` ochie. Ha niile bụ nhọrọ — ndabara VNC na-ezo aka na image `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` e tinyere n'ime ngwugwu, a na-agbanwekwa ha naanị maka image container ahaziri iche, port, ma ọ bụ nhazi okirikiri ndụ.

| Agbanwe                               | Ndabara                                                                  | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tag image Docker maka container nbanye Chromium+VNC. Wuo `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` ma ọ bụ mee ka nke a zoo aka na image ahaziri iche.                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Binary runtime container a na-eji ebido container VNC (dịka ọmụmaatụ, tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `podman`).                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port VNC/noVNC e kpughere n'ime container.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port Chrome DevTools Protocol dị n'ime container.                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ụzọ directory profaịlụ Chromium dị n'ime container.                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Directory host nke na-edobe profaịlụ nbanye ihe nchọgharị echekwara.                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (nkeji 10)                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge nkwụsị n'ihi enweghị ọrụ (ms) tupu ewepụ oge VNC na-adịghị arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (nkeji 30)                                                     | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oke siri ike (ms) maka ogologo ndụ otu oge VNC.                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọnụọgụ kachasị elu nke oge VNC nwere ike ịrụ ọrụ n'otu oge.                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge nkwụsị (ms) maka ichere ka ihe nchọgharị dị n'ime container dị njikere maka CDP.                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge nkwụsị (ms) maka iwepụta oge/cookies e jidere mgbe nbanye zuru ezu.                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Flags command-line ndị ọzọ a na-enyefe Chromium dị n'ime container.                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Netwọkụ Docker pụrụ iche nke container nbanye VNC na-esonye (#12571) kama bridge ndabara, ka containers ibe ya ghara iru port bridge CDP ya.                                                   |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(edoghị)_                                                               | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Utuaha ochie** maka `DATA_DIR`, nke a na-enyocha naanị mgbe edoghị ma `DATA_DIR` ma `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`. Ọ na-achọta cache oge web-thread Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

## 26. Nnwale & Ngwaọrụ E2E

Ndị a bụ ndị `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`,
`scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, na `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs` na-eji. Ahapụla
uru niile dị n'okpuru n'enweghị nhazi na mwepụta prodọkshọn.

| Mgbanwe                                | Ndabara                                          | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                                           | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Ọnọdụ mmalite E2E (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) maka onye na-agba Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | na-eji `INITIAL_PASSWORD` ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Okwuntughe onye nchịkwa a na-etinye n'ime gburugburu Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                                           | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Gbanyụọ nyocha ugboro ugboro nke healthcheck mpaghara mgbe Playwright na-agba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                                           | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Gbanyụọ usoro nyocha ugboro ugboro nke healthcheck token OAuth n'oge ule.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(edoghị ya)_                                    | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Ndị na-eweta ọrụ, nke e ji rikoma kewaa, a ga-ewepụ na nyocha mweghachi token a na-eme tupu oge eruo (dịka `codex,openai`). Ọ bụ ụzọ ọzọ ezubere iche kama ịgbanyụ healthcheck kpamkpam — ndị na-eweta ọrụ nwere TTL dị mkpụmkpụ ga-anọgide na-emelite, ebe ndị na-eweta cascade ga-emeghachi omume naanị mgbe ọ dị mkpa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                                           | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Mechie mkpọtụ healthcheck na stdout nke Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Mafere mwube prodọkshọn Next.js tupu Playwright amalite (njikarịcha CI).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                                              | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Mafere hook iwepụ OmniRoute (CI na-eji ya edobe `node_modules` ka ọ ghara ịgbanwe).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                                         | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Oge nchere (ms) ka sava nwee ezi ọnọdụ tupu ịgba ule ecosystem/protocol.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login`                   | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL nke ngwaọrụ smoke Electron na-atụ anya ka ngwa a kwakọtara nye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                                          | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ngụkọta oge ngwụcha (ms) tupu ngwaọrụ smoke kwụsị ịnwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                                           | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Oge nkwụsị (ms) mgbe ibe ahụ kwụsịrị ibudata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(akpaka)_                                       | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ụzọ akọwapụtara nke faịlụ executable Electron a kwakọtara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                                       | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ndekọ data maka ịgba ule smoke Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                                              | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji chekwaa ndekọ data smoke mgbe arụmọrụ ahụ gachara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                                              | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji zipụ ndekọ Electron ozugbo na stdout n'oge arụmọrụ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                                              | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: malitegharịa site n'iji otu ndekọ data ahụ ma gosi na mmalite nke abụọ họọrọ draịva SQLite nke sistemụ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(nchọgharị PATH)_                               | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Dochie ụzọ faịlụ binari Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

### Usoro ntụgharị asụsụ dọkụmentị

Nke `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` na-eji (iwu `npm run i18n:run`).
Agbanwebeghị mgbanwe ise niile na ndabara — tọọ ha na `.env` naanị na kọmputa
ndị kwesịrị inwe ike ịrụ onye ntụgharị dọkụmentị ahụ.

| Mgbanwe                             | Ndabara      | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                  | Nkọwa                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`     | _(atọbeghị)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | URL ntọala kwekọrọ na OpenAI maka backend ntụgharị asụsụ.                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`     | _(atọbeghị)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Token Bearer maka backend ntụgharị asụsụ (anaghị edekọ ya ma ọlị).                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`       | _(atọbeghị)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | NJ model, dịka `gpt-4o-mini` ma ọ bụ `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `60000`      | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Oge ngwụcha arịrịọ ọ bụla na milisekọnd.                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY` | `4`          | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Arịrịọ ntụgharị asụsụ ndị na-aga n'otu oge mgbe a na-arụ ọrụ n'elu ọtụtụ faịlụ / mpaghara asụsụ. |
